```
     1→import asyncio
     2→import json
     3→import logging
     4→from typing import AsyncIterator
     5→
     6→from claude_agent_sdk import AgentDefinition
     7→from app.database import Database
     8→from app.config import (
     9→    ORCHESTRATOR_MODEL_SDK,
    10→    SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL_SDK,
    11→    BACKEND_BASE_URL,
    12→    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY, LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY, LANGFUSE_BASE_URL, LANGFUSE_ENABLED,
    13→    SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS,
    14→)
    15→from app.memory_store import memory_store
    16→from app.agent_memory import read_memories
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt(db: Database) -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→
    25→Tools at your disposal:
    26→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql — run SQL queries against DuckDB
    27→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__render_chart — render a Plotly chart
    28→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question — ask the user a clarifying question
    29→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__create_skill — create a reusable skill (workflow template) that can be invoked later via /skill-name
    30→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__save_memory — save a fact, preference, or pattern to long-term memory
    31→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__recall_memories — retrieve stored memories
    32→- mcp__duckdb-data-agent__forget_memory — remove a specific memory
    33→- Task tool with subagent_type "sql-analyst" — delegate complex multi-query data exploration
    34→
    35→Skill creation workflow (follow this exactly):
    36→When the user asks you to create a skill, do NOT call create_skill immediately. Follow this process:
    37→1. If the user's request is vague or unclear about what the skill should do, use ask_user_question to clarify the skill's purpose, scope, and key steps BEFORE drafting any plan. Keep asking until you have enough detail.
    38→2. Once the requirements are clear, draft a plan for the skill: propose a name, description, and outline of the content (step-by-step instructions).
    39→3. Present the plan to the user using ask_user_question with options like "Looks good, create it" and "I want to make changes". Describe the full plan in the question text.
    40→4. If the user wants changes, update the plan based on their feedback and present the revised plan again (repeat step 3).
    41→5. Only after the user explicitly approves the plan, call create_skill with the finalized name, description, and content.
    42→- NEVER skip the review step. The user must approve before the skill is created.
    43→
    44→Task tool usage:
    45→- When using the Task tool, set "subagent_type" to "sql-analyst". NEVER use generic values like "general-purpose" or any other value.
    46→- Do NOT set the "model" parameter on the Task tool — it is already configured.
    47→- After the sql-analyst returns, do NOT repeat the data tables, numbers, or SQL queries. Simply add brief commentary on what the data means. Never include SQL in your answer unless the user explicitly asks to see the SQL.
    48→
    49→Direct tool usage:
    50→- For simple SQL queries, call execute_sql directly instead of delegating to sql-analyst.
    51→- For charts/visualizations, call execute_sql to get the data, then call render_chart yourself with the Plotly spec. Do NOT delegate charting to a subagent.
    52→
    53→Charting workflow (follow this exactly):
    54→1. Run execute_sql to get the data you need for a chart.
    55→2. Call render_chart with TWO required parameters:
    56→   - `data`: array of Plotly trace objects (e.g. [{"type": "bar", "x": [...], "y": [...]}])
    57→   - `layout`: object that MUST include `title` (e.g. {"title": "My Chart"})
    58→   Both `data` and `layout` are required — the tool WILL accept both. Do not second-guess this.
    59→3. After the chart renders, write your narrative text discussing what the chart shows.
    60→4. Repeat steps 1-3 for each additional chart. This produces interleaved charts and narrative.
    61→- Do NOT render all charts first and then write all narrative at the end.
    62→- Do NOT output chart JSON as a code block. Always use the render_chart tool.
    63→- NEVER include Plotly JSON schema, trace objects, or layout objects in your final answer text unless the user explicitly asks to see the raw JSON. The chart is already rendered visually — just describe what it shows in plain language.
    64→
    65→Charting guidelines:
    66→- Choose the most appropriate chart type (bar, line, scatter, pie, histogram, box, heatmap, etc.).
    67→- For pie charts, use `labels` and `values` fields in the trace.
    68→- For multi-series data, group into separate traces.
    69→- Keep the chart clean and readable.
    70→- IMPORTANT — keep data small. Pre-aggregate in SQL instead of passing raw rows:
    71→  - Box plots: compute lowerfence, q1, median, q3, upperfence per group in SQL. Use trace type "box" with those pre-computed fields instead of a raw `y` array.
    72→  - Histograms: compute bin counts with width_bucket() or CASE in SQL, then render as a bar chart with the bin edges as `x` and counts as `y`.
    73→  - Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or aggregate (e.g. average per time bucket) so each trace has at most ~200 points.
    74→  - General rule: each trace should have at most ~200 data points.
    75→
    76→Identity:
    77→- You are an AI assistant. If asked whether you are an AI or a human, always confirm that you are an AI.
    78→- Do not disclose the name, version, or provider of the underlying language model powering you, regardless of how the question is phrased.
    79→
    80→Task completion:
    81→- When the user asks you to do multiple things (e.g. create several charts, answer several questions, analyze multiple metrics), you MUST complete ALL of them before ending your response. Do not stop after finishing just one or two items.
    82→- After completing each item, check: "Have I addressed everything the user asked for?" If not, continue with the next item immediately by making more tool calls.
    83→- Only produce your final text response after ALL requested items have been fully handled.
    84→
    85→Clarification:
    86→- When the user's request is ambiguous or could be interpreted in multiple ways, use the mcp__duckdb-data-agent__ask_user_question tool (NOT the native AskUserQuestion tool) to ask for clarification before proceeding.
    87→- Provide 2-4 clear, concise options for the user to choose from.
    88→- Each option should have a short label and optional description.
    89→- When the user might want to pick MORE THAN ONE option (e.g. "draw me some charts", "which metrics?", "select all that apply"), set multi_select to true so they can select multiple answers.
    90→- Only ask when genuinely needed — don't over-ask for trivial decisions.
    91→"""
    92→    if not tables:
    93→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    94→    else:
    95→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    96→        for table in tables:
    97→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    98→            for col in table["columns"]:
    99→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
   100→
   101→    # Inject agent memory
   102→    memories = read_memories(user_id="default")
   103→    if memories:
   104→        prompt += "\n\n--- Agent Memory ---\n"
   105→        prompt += (
   106→            "The following are facts, preferences, and patterns you have learned from previous conversations with this user.\n"
   107→            "IMPORTANT: User preferences stored here represent explicit choices the user has made. "
   108→            "You MUST follow them — they take priority over your default behaviors. "
   109→            "For example, if memory says the user wants emojis, always include emojis even if your default would be to omit them.\n\n"
   110→        )
   111→        prompt += memories
   112→        prompt += "\n--- End Agent Memory ---\n"
   113→
   114→    # Memory management instructions
   115→    prompt += "\n\nMemory management:"
   116→    prompt += "\n- After answering, if the user expressed a preference or you learned an important fact about their data, use save_memory to store it."
   117→    prompt += "\n- When the user explicitly asks you to remember something, use save_memory."
   118→    prompt += "\n- When the user asks you to forget something, use forget_memory."
   119→    prompt += "\n- Do not save trivial or obvious information. Only save things that would be useful in future conversations."
   120→
   121→    return prompt
   122→
   123→
   124→def _build_table_schemas(db: Database) -> str:
   125→    """Build a text description of all loaded table schemas for subagent prompts."""
   126→    tables = db.list_tables()
   127→    if not tables:
   128→        return "\nNo tables are currently loaded."
   129→    text = "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
   130→    for table in tables:
   131→        text += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
   132→        for col in table["columns"]:
   133→            text += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
   134→    return text
   135→
   136→
   137→def build_subagent_definitions(db: Database) -> dict[str, AgentDefinition]:
   138→    """Build AgentDefinition objects for subagents."""
   139→    table_schemas = _build_table_schemas(db)
   140→
   141→    sql_prompt = (
   142→        "You are a DuckDB SQL expert. Given a user's data question, write and execute "
   143→        "SQL queries to find the answer.\n\n"
   144→        "Guidelines:\n"
   145→        "- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries.\n"
   146→        "- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT).\n"
   147→        "- If a query fails, read the error message, fix the SQL, and retry.\n"
   148→        "- Use double quotes for table and column identifiers that might conflict "
   149→        "with reserved words.\n"
   150→        "- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results.\n"
   151→        "- Do NOT include SQL queries you wrote in your final answer unless the user explicitly "
   152→        "asks to see the SQL. Focus on the results and insights.\n"
   153→        + table_schemas
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    return {
   157→        "sql-analyst": AgentDefinition(
   158→            description=(
   159→                "Use this agent for complex data exploration that requires multiple "
   160→                "SQL queries with iterative refinement — e.g. exploring an unfamiliar "
   161→                "dataset, multi-step aggregations, or debugging query errors. "
   162→                "For simple one-off queries, call execute_sql directly instead."
   163→            ),
   164→            prompt=sql_prompt,
   165→            tools=["mcp__duckdb-data-agent__execute_sql"],
   166→            model=SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL_SDK,
   167→        ),
   168→    }
   169→
   170→
   171→def _build_message_with_history(
   172→    message: str, conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None
   173→) -> str:
   174→    """Prepend conversation history context to the user message when editing."""
   175→    if not conversation_history:
   176→        return message
   177→
   178→    history_text = "Previous conversation (for context, I am now editing a message):\n"
   179→    for entry in conversation_history:
   180→        role = entry.get("role", "user").capitalize()
   181→        content = entry.get("content", "")
   182→        history_text += f"\n{role}: {content}\n"
   183→    history_text += f"\n---\n\nMy updated message:\n{message}"
   184→    return history_text
   185→
   186→
   187→
   188→async def stream_chat(
   189→    message: str,
   190→    session_id: str | None = None,
   191→    db: Database | None = None,
   192→    conversation_history: list[dict] | None = None,
   193→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None,
   194→    backend_session_id: str | None = None,
   195→    skills: list[str] | None = None,
   196→    conversation_id: str | None = None,
   197→) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
   198→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events via containerized sidecar."""
   199→    # Persist user message if conversation_id provided
   200→    if conversation_id:
   201→        try:
   202→            memory_store.add_message(conversation_id, "user", message)
   203→        except Exception:
   204→            logger.warning("Failed to persist user message", exc_info=True)
   205→
   206→    from app.container_manager import container_manager
   207→    if container_manager is None:
   208→        raise RuntimeError("Docker is not available. Container mode requires Docker.")
   209→    if db is None:
   210→        raise ValueError("db must be provided")
   211→
   212→    import httpx
   213→    import asyncio
   214→
   215→    # Only bake history into message text for edit/delete (no session to resume).
   216→    # For normal follow-ups with a session_id, pass history as a separate field
   217→    # so the sidecar can use it as fallback if resume fails.
   218→    query_message = _build_message_with_history(message, conversation_history) if not session_id else message
   219→    system_prompt = build_system_prompt(db)
   220→
   221→    # Pass Langfuse credentials to the container so the sidecar's
   222→    # TypeScript Langfuse SDK can create traces directly.
   223→    env: dict[str, str] = {
   224→        "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   225→        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   226→        "SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS": str(SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS),
   227→    }
   228→    if LANGFUSE_ENABLED:
   229→        env["LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY
   230→        env["LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY"] = LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY
   231→        env["LANGFUSE_BASE_URL"] = LANGFUSE_BASE_URL
   232→
   233→    # Use the backend session ID (X-Session-ID header) for both:
   234→    # 1. MCP SSE URL — so the container queries the correct DuckDB instance
   235→    # 2. Container lifecycle key — so the same container is reused across
   236→    #    requests from the same browser tab (the Claude agent session_id
   237→    #    changes after the first response, which would orphan the container)
   238→    stable_session = backend_session_id or session_id or "default"
   239→
   240→    # Declare persistence variables before try so they're accessible in finally
   241→    assistant_text_parts: list[str] = []
   242→    all_text_parts: list[str] = []
   243→    assistant_metadata: dict = {"sql_queries": [], "chart_specs": []}
   244→    persisted_segments: list[dict] = []
   245→    seg_thinking_buf: list[str] = []
   246→    seg_answer_buf: list[str] = []
   247→    seg_phase = "thinking"
   248→    actual_session_id = session_id
   249→
   250→    try:
   251→        # Send SSE keepalive immediately so the HTTP response starts and
   252→        # intermediate proxies (Vite, nginx) don't drop the idle connection
   253→        # before we've finished the blocking Docker container creation.
   254→        yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   255→
   256→        # container_manager.create() is synchronous (blocking Docker API call).
   257→        # Run it in a thread executor so the event loop stays responsive and
   258→        # can continue flushing keepalives to the client during startup.
   259→        # gVisor (runsc) containers can take 10-30 seconds to spin up.
   260→        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   261→        create_future = loop.run_in_executor(None, container_manager.create, stable_session, env)
   262→
   263→        max_create_wait = 60.0
   264→        elapsed = 0.0
   265→        while not create_future.done():
   266→            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
   267→            elapsed += 2.0
   268→            if elapsed >= max_create_wait:
   269→                create_future.cancel()
   270→                raise RuntimeError(f"Container creation timed out after {max_create_wait:.0f}s")
   271→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   272→        info = await create_future
   273→        container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   274→
   275→        # Wait for container to be ready
   276→        for attempt in range(10):
   277→            try:
   278→                async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(5.0)) as check_client:
   279→                    resp = await check_client.get(f"{info.url}/health")
   280→                    if resp.status_code == 200:
   281→                        break
   282→            except Exception:
   283→                pass
   284→            yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   285→            await asyncio.sleep(1)
   286→        else:
   287→            raise RuntimeError("Sidecar container failed health check after 10 attempts")
   288→
   289→        payload: dict = {
   290→            "message": query_message,
   291→            "session_id": session_id,
   292→            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   293→            "model": ORCHESTRATOR_MODEL_SDK,
   294→            "mcp_server_url": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/mcp/sse?session_id={stable_session}",
   295→            "env": {
   296→                "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "placeholder",
   297→                "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"{BACKEND_BASE_URL}/anthropic",
   298→            },
   299→            "agents": {
   300→                name: {
   301→                    "description": agent_def.description,
   302→                    "prompt": agent_def.prompt,
   303→                    "tools": agent_def.tools,
   304→                    "model": agent_def.model,
   305→                }
   306→                for name, agent_def in build_subagent_definitions(db).items()
   307→            },
   308→        }
   309→        if langfuse_session_id:
   310→            payload["langfuse_session_id"] = langfuse_session_id
   311→        # Always pass conversation_history so the sidecar can use it as fallback
   312→        # if resume fails (e.g. container was recreated and session is gone).
   313→        # Also pass original_message for Langfuse trace metadata.
   314→        if conversation_history:
   315→            payload["original_message"] = message
   316→        payload["conversation_history"] = conversation_history or []
   317→        if skills:
   318→            payload["skills"] = skills
   319→
   320→        has_tool_calls = False
   321→        has_thinking = False
   322→        done_sent = False
   323→        waiting_for_user = False
   324→        tool_names: dict[str, str] = {}
   325→        tool_sqls: dict[str, str] = {}
   326→        # Capture render_chart tool_use inputs so we can attach chart_spec
   327→        # to the tool_result event (the MCP tool result itself may not echo
   328→        # the full spec).
   329→        tool_chart_specs: dict[str, dict] = {}
   330→        # Track subagent text output from intermediate assistant messages.
   331→        # The TypeScript SDK's Task tool_result contains only metadata (agentId,
   332→        # usage), not the subagent's actual output.  The real output arrives in
   333→        # assistant messages whose parent_tool_use_id matches the Task tool ID.
   334→        subagent_texts: dict[str, str] = {}
   335→        # Track SQL queries executed inside subagents so they can be
   336→        # included in the subagent_end event for visibility.
   337→        subagent_sql_data: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}  # parent_tool_use_id -> [{tool_id, sql, columns?, rows?, rowCount?}]
   338→        subagent_internal_tools: dict[str, str] = {}   # tool_id -> parent_tool_use_id
   339→
   340→        # Timeout must exceed SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS so the sidecar's own
   341→        # idle abort fires first; add a 60s buffer.
   342→        httpx_timeout = (SDK_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000) + 60
   343→        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(httpx_timeout)) as client:
   344→            async with client.stream("POST", f"{info.url}/query", json=payload) as response:
   345→                line_iter = response.aiter_lines().__aiter__()
   346→                while True:
   347→                    try:
   348→                        if waiting_for_user:
   349→                            try:
   350→                                line = await asyncio.wait_for(line_iter.__anext__(), timeout=5.0)
   351→                            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   352→                                yield ": keepalive\n\n"
   353→                                continue
   354→                        else:
   355→                            line = await line_iter.__anext__()
   356→                    except StopAsyncIteration:
   357→                        break
   358→                    except httpx.RemoteProtocolError:
   359→                        # Sidecar closed the connection before sending a
   360→                        # proper chunked-encoding terminator.  This is
   361→                        # benign — the sidecar's Express handler calls
   362→                        # res.end() in its finally block and the
   363→                        # connection can tear down before the last chunk
   364→                        # delimiter reaches us.  Treat it as end-of-stream.
   365→                        logger.warning("Sidecar closed connection early (incomplete chunked read); treating as end-of-stream")
   366→                        break
   367→
   368→                    if not line.startswith("data: "):
   369→                        continue
   370→                    raw = line[6:]
   371→                    waiting_for_user = False
   372→                    try:
   373→                        msg = json.loads(raw)
   374→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   375→                        continue
   376→
   377→                    # Reset container idle timer on every message so
   378→                    # long-running queries don't trigger the reaper.
   379→                    container_manager.touch(stable_session)
   380→
   381→                    msg_type = msg.get("type")
   382→                    # --- Token-level streaming events from SDK ---
   383→                    if msg_type == "stream_event":
   384→                        event = msg.get("event", {})
   385→                        event_type = event.get("type", "")
   386→                        stream_parent = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   387→                        is_subagent_event = bool(stream_parent and stream_parent in tool_names)
   388→
   389→                        if event_type == "content_block_delta":
   390→                            delta = event.get("delta", {})
   391→                            delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
   392→                            if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
   393→                                text = delta.get("thinking", "")
   394→                                if text and not is_subagent_event:
   395→                                    seg_thinking_buf.append(text)
   396→                                    yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   397→                            elif delta_type == "text_delta":
   398→                                text = delta.get("text", "")
   399→                                if text:
   400→                                    # Accumulate subagent text for chart_spec
   401→                                    # extraction (the SDK may not yield a
   402→                                    # complete assistant message for the
   403→                                    # subagent's final turn).
   404→                                    if is_subagent_event:
   405→                                        subagent_texts[stream_parent] = subagent_texts.get(stream_parent, "") + text
   406→                                    else:
   407→                                        event_name = "answer" if has_tool_calls else "thinking"
   408→                                        all_text_parts.append(text)
   409→                                        # Track text in segment buffers based on actual phase
   410→                                        if seg_phase == "thinking":
   411→                                            seg_thinking_buf.append(text)
   412→                                        else:
   413→                                            seg_answer_buf.append(text)
   414→                                        if event_name == "answer":
   415→                                            assistant_text_parts.append(text)
   416→                                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
   417→
   418→                        elif event_type == "content_block_start":
   419→                            block = event.get("content_block", {})
   420→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   421→                            if block_type == "thinking":
   422→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   423→                                    has_thinking = True
   424→                            elif block_type == "text":
   425→                                if has_thinking and not is_subagent_event:
   426→                                    # Flush thinking buffer into a segment
   427→                                    if seg_thinking_buf:
   428→                                        persisted_segments.append({"type": "thinking", "text": "".join(seg_thinking_buf)})
   429→                                        seg_thinking_buf.clear()
   430→                                    seg_phase = "answer"
   431→                                    yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   432→                            elif block_type == "tool_use":
   433→                                if not is_subagent_event:
   434→                                    # Flush thinking buffer before tool calls
   435→                                    if seg_thinking_buf:
   436→                                        persisted_segments.append({"type": "thinking", "text": "".join(seg_thinking_buf)})
   437→                                        seg_thinking_buf.clear()
   438→                                    seg_phase = "answer"
   439→                                    has_thinking = False
   440→                                    has_tool_calls = True
   441→
   442→                    # --- Complete assistant message (contains tool_use blocks) ---
   443→                    elif msg_type == "assistant":
   444→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   445→                        parent_tool_use_id = msg.get("parent_tool_use_id")
   446→
   447→                        # Capture text from subagent assistant messages.
   448→                        # These carry the real subagent output (e.g. chart JSON)
   449→                        # that the Task tool_result metadata lacks.
   450→                        if parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names:
   451→                            text_parts = []
   452→                            for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   453→                                if block.get("type") == "text" and block.get("text"):
   454→                                    text_parts.append(block["text"])
   455→                            if text_parts:
   456→                                subagent_texts[parent_tool_use_id] = "\n".join(text_parts)
   457→
   458→                        is_subagent_msg = bool(parent_tool_use_id and parent_tool_use_id in tool_names)
   459→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   460→                            block_type = block.get("type")
   461→                            if block_type == "tool_use":
   462→                                tool_id = block.get("id", "")
   463→                                tool_name = block.get("name", "")
   464→                                tool_input = block.get("input", {})
   465→                                tool_names[tool_id] = tool_name
   466→
   467→                                # Skip emitting tool_call for subagent-internal
   468→                                # tools (execute_sql inside sql-analyst).  The
   469→                                # SDK doesn't yield the matching tool_result
   470→                                # user message, so the frontend would show
   471→                                # "Executing..." forever.
   472→                                if is_subagent_msg:
   473→                                    subagent_internal_tools[tool_id] = parent_tool_use_id
   474→                                    # Capture SQL for subagent_end visibility
   475→                                    if "execute_sql" in tool_name:
   476→                                        sql = tool_input.get("sql", "")
   477→                                        if sql:
   478→                                            subagent_sql_data.setdefault(parent_tool_use_id, []).append(
   479→                                                {"tool_id": tool_id, "sql": sql}
   480→                                            )
   481→                                    continue
   482→
   483→                                has_tool_calls = True
   484→                                is_execute_sql = "execute_sql" in tool_name
   485→                                is_render_chart = "render_chart" in tool_name
   486→                                sql = tool_input.get("sql", "") if is_execute_sql else ""
   487→                                if sql:
   488→                                    tool_sqls[tool_id] = sql
   489→                                # Capture render_chart input so we can attach
   490→                                # chart_spec to the tool_result event.
   491→                                if is_render_chart:
   492→                                    tool_chart_specs[tool_id] = tool_input
   493→                                tool_call_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": tool_name}
   494→                                if sql:
   495→                                    tool_call_data["sql"] = sql
   496→                                else:
   497→                                    tool_call_data["input"] = tool_input
   498→                                yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps(tool_call_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   499→                                # Track tool segment for persistence (placeholder, updated on result)
   500→                                persisted_segments.append({
   501→                                    "type": "tool",
   502→                                    "toolCallId": tool_id,
   503→                                    "toolName": tool_name,
   504→                                    "sql": sql or None,
   505→                                })
   506→                                if tool_name == "Task":
   507→                                    subagent_name = tool_input.get("subagent_type", "unknown")
   508→                                    subagent_prompt = tool_input.get("prompt", "")
   509→                                    tool_names[tool_id] = subagent_name
   510→                                    # Replace tool segment with subagent_start for subagent tools
   511→                                    persisted_segments[-1] = {
   512→                                        "type": "subagent_start",
   513→                                        "subagentId": tool_id,
   514→                                        "subagentName": subagent_name,
   515→                                    }
   516→                                    yield f"event: subagent_start\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': tool_id, 'name': subagent_name, 'prompt': subagent_prompt})}\n\n"
   517→
   518→                                # Detect ask_user_question tool
   519→                                if "ask_user_question" in tool_name:
   520→                                    from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store
   521→                                    import asyncio as _asyncio
   522→                                    for _ in range(50):
   523→                                        pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(stable_session)
   524→                                        if pending:
   525→                                            yield f"event: user_question\ndata: {json.dumps({'question_id': pending['question_id'], **pending['data']})}\n\n"
   526→                                            waiting_for_user = True
   527→                                            break
   528→                                        await _asyncio.sleep(0.1)
   529→
   530→                    # --- Tool results from user messages ---
   531→                    elif msg_type == "user":
   532→                        message_obj = msg.get("message", {})
   533→                        # The SDK attaches the subagent's actual output in
   534→                        # tool_use_result.content (a list of content blocks).
   535→                        # Extract it so we can use it for chart_spec extraction.
   536→                        tool_use_result = msg.get("tool_use_result")
   537→                        tool_use_result_text = ""
   538→                        if isinstance(tool_use_result, dict):
   539→                            tur_content = tool_use_result.get("content")
   540→                            if isinstance(tur_content, list):
   541→                                parts = []
   542→                                for item in tur_content:
   543→                                    if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") == "text":
   544→                                        parts.append(item.get("text", ""))
   545→                                tool_use_result_text = "\n".join(parts)
   546→                        for block in message_obj.get("content", []):
   547→                            if block.get("type") != "tool_result":
   548→                                continue
   549→                            tool_id = block.get("tool_use_id", "")
   550→
   551→                            # Skip subagent-internal tool results; capture
   552→                            # structured data for inclusion in subagent_end.
   553→                            parent_subagent_id = subagent_internal_tools.get(tool_id)
   554→                            if parent_subagent_id:
   555→                                sa_name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")
   556→                                if "execute_sql" in sa_name:
   557→                                    sa_parts = block.get("content", [])
   558→                                    sa_text = ""
   559→                                    if isinstance(sa_parts, list):
   560→                                        for part in sa_parts:
   561→                                            if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text":
   562→                                                sa_text = part.get("text", "")
   563→                                    elif isinstance(sa_parts, str):
   564→                                        sa_text = sa_parts
   565→                                    try:
   566→                                        sa_parsed = json.loads(sa_text)
   567→                                        for entry in subagent_sql_data.get(parent_subagent_id, []):
   568→                                            if entry.get("tool_id") == tool_id:
   569→                                                if sa_parsed.get("status") == "success":
   570→                                                    entry["columns"] = sa_parsed.get("columns", [])
   571→                                                    entry["rows"] = sa_parsed.get("rows", [])[:100]
   572→                                                    entry["rowCount"] = sa_parsed.get("rowCount", 0)
   573→                                                elif sa_parsed.get("status") == "error":
   574→                                                    entry["error"] = sa_parsed.get("error", "")
   575→                                                break
   576→                                    except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   577→                                        pass
   578→                                continue
   579→
   580→                            name = tool_names.get(tool_id, "")
   581→                            content_parts = block.get("content", [])
   582→                            text = ""
   583→                            if isinstance(content_parts, list):
   584→                                for part in content_parts:
   585→                                    if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get("type") == "text":
   586→                                        text = part.get("text", "")
   587→                            elif isinstance(content_parts, str):
   588→                                text = content_parts
   589→
   590→                            # Try to parse structured MCP result
   591→                            result_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   592→                            # Include the SQL from the original tool_call
   593→                            original_sql = tool_sqls.get(tool_id, "")
   594→                            if original_sql:
   595→                                result_data["sql"] = original_sql
   596→                            try:
   597→                                parsed = json.loads(text)
   598→                                if parsed.get("status") == "success":
   599→                                    if "chart_spec" in parsed:
   600→                                        result_data["chart_spec"] = parsed["chart_spec"]
   601→                                    else:
   602→                                        result_data["columns"] = parsed.get("columns", [])
   603→                                        result_data["rows"] = parsed.get("rows", [])[:100]
   604→                                        result_data["rowCount"] = parsed.get("rowCount", 0)
   605→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":
   606→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed.get("error", "")
   607→                                else:
   608→                                    result_data["output"] = text
   609→                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   610→                                result_data["output"] = text
   611→                            if block.get("is_error"):
   612→                                try:
   613→                                    parsed_err = json.loads(text)
   614→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed_err.get("error", text)
   615→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   616→                                    result_data["error"] = text
   617→                            # Attach chart_spec from captured render_chart
   618→                            # input so the frontend can render the chart.
   619→                            # Only attach when the tool returned success — do
   620→                            # NOT render a chart when the tool returned an
   621→                            # error (e.g. empty traces), as the LLM expects
   622→                            # the chart to have failed and may put its
   623→                            # analysis in thinking instead of text output.
   624→                            if tool_id in tool_chart_specs and "chart_spec" not in result_data and "error" not in result_data:
   625→                                result_data["chart_spec"] = tool_chart_specs[tool_id]
   626→                            # Detect subagent result (Task tool)
   627→                            if name == "sql-analyst":
   628→                                end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   629→                                sql_data = subagent_sql_data.get(tool_id, [])
   630→                                if sql_data:
   631→                                    end_data["sql_results"] = [
   632→                                        {k: v for k, v in entry.items() if k != "tool_id"}
   633→                                        for entry in sql_data
   634→                                    ]
   635→                                narrative = subagent_texts.get(tool_id, "") or tool_use_result_text
   636→                                if narrative:
   637→                                    end_data["result"] = narrative
   638→                                # Track subagent_end segment for persistence
   639→                                persisted_segments.append({
   640→                                    "type": "subagent_end",
   641→                                    "subagentId": tool_id,
   642→                                    "subagentName": name,
   643→                                    "text": narrative or None,
   644→                                    "sqlResults": end_data.get("sql_results"),
   645→                                })
   646→                                yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   647→                                continue
   648→                            # Accumulate SQL and chart data for persistence
   649→                            if "columns" in result_data and "rows" in result_data:
   650→                                assistant_metadata["sql_queries"].append({
   651→                                    "sql": result_data.get("sql", ""),
   652→                                    "columns": result_data.get("columns", []),
   653→                                    "rowCount": result_data.get("rowCount", 0),
   654→                                })
   655→                            if "chart_spec" in result_data:
   656→                                assistant_metadata["chart_specs"].append(result_data["chart_spec"])
   657→                            # Update tool segment with result summary
   658→                            for seg in persisted_segments:
   659→                                if seg.get("type") == "tool" and seg.get("toolCallId") == tool_id:
   660→                                    seg["rowCount"] = result_data.get("rowCount")
   661→                                    seg["error"] = result_data.get("error")
   662→                                    if "chart_spec" in result_data:
   663→                                        seg["chart_spec"] = result_data["chart_spec"]
   664→                                    break
   665→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   666→
   667→                    # --- Final result ---
   668→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   669→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   670→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   671→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   672→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   673→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   674→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   675→                        done_sent = True
   676→
   677→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   678→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   679→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   680→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   681→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   682→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   683→                        done_sent = True
   684→
   685→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   686→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   687→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   688→                        if sys_session:
   689→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   690→
   691→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   692→        if not done_sent:
   693→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   694→            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
   695→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   696→
   697→    except Exception as e:
   698→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   699→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   700→    finally:
   701→        # Persist assistant message even when the client disconnects
   702→        # (e.g. user switched conversations mid-stream).  Without this,
   703→        # the assistant response would be lost on reload.
   704→        text_to_persist = assistant_text_parts or all_text_parts
   705→        if conversation_id and text_to_persist:
   706→            try:
   707→                # Finalize segments: flush remaining buffers
   708→                if seg_thinking_buf:
   709→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "thinking", "text": "".join(seg_thinking_buf)})
   710→                    seg_thinking_buf.clear()
   711→                # Answer text: use seg_answer_buf (tracks post-thinking text)
   712→                # or assistant_text_parts (tracks post-tool text), or fallback
   713→                answer_text = "".join(seg_answer_buf) if seg_answer_buf else "".join(assistant_text_parts)
   714→                if answer_text:
   715→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "answer", "text": answer_text})
   716→                elif all_text_parts and not persisted_segments:
   717→                    # No thinking blocks, no tool calls — all text is the answer
   718→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "answer", "text": "".join(all_text_parts)})
   719→
   720→                meta = assistant_metadata.copy() if (assistant_metadata["sql_queries"] or assistant_metadata["chart_specs"]) else {}
   721→                if persisted_segments:
   722→                    meta["segments"] = persisted_segments
   723→                memory_store.add_message(
   724→                    conversation_id, "assistant", "".join(text_to_persist),
   725→                    metadata=meta if meta else None,
   726→                )
   727→            except Exception:
   728→                logger.warning("Failed to persist assistant message", exc_info=True)
   729→    # Note: Container is kept alive for session resume (--resume flag) and is
   730→    # cleaned up by the background cleanup loop after
   731→    # CONTAINER_MAX_LIFETIME_SECONDS, or on application shutdown.
   732→
```

```
    31→def _create_mcp_server(db: Database, session_id: str) -> MCPServer:
    32→    """Create a duckdb-data-agent MCP server with execute_sql, ask_user_question, and render_chart tools bound to a DuckDB instance."""

    35→    @server.list_tools()

    43→                ),

    45→                    "type": "object",

    78→                            ),
    79→                        },

    84→            types.Tool(

   115→                ),

   126→                        },

   130→                        },
   131→                    },
   132→                    "required": ["name", "description", "content"],
   133→                },
   134→            ),
   135→            types.Tool(
   136→                name="save_memory",
   137→                description=(
   138→                    "Save a piece of information to long-term memory so it persists across conversations. "
   139→                    "Use this to remember user preferences, important facts, or recurring patterns."
   140→                ),
   141→                inputSchema={
   142→                    "type": "object",
   143→                    "properties": {
   144→                        "content": {
   145→                            "type": "string",
   146→                            "description": "The memory content to save (e.g. 'User prefers bar charts')",
   147→                        },
   148→                        "category": {
   149→                            "type": "string",
   150→                            "enum": ["preference", "fact", "pattern"],
   151→                            "description": "Category of the memory: preference (user likes/dislikes), fact (important information), or pattern (recurring behavior)",
   152→                        },
   153→                    },
   154→                    "required": ["content", "category"],
   155→                },
   156→            ),
   157→            types.Tool(
   158→                name="recall_memories",
   159→                description=(
   160→                    "Recall all saved memories, optionally filtered by a keyword query. "
   161→                    "Use this at the start of a conversation to check for relevant context."
   162→                ),
   163→                inputSchema={
   164→                    "type": "object",
   165→                    "properties": {
   166→                        "query": {
   167→                            "type": "string",
   168→                            "description": "Optional keyword to filter memories (case-insensitive substring match)",
   169→                        },
   170→                    },
   171→                },
   172→            ),
   173→            types.Tool(
   174→                name="forget_memory",
   175→                description=(
   176→                    "Remove a specific memory entry. The content must match an existing memory exactly."
   177→                ),
   178→                inputSchema={
   179→                    "type": "object",
   180→                    "properties": {
   181→                        "content": {
   182→                            "type": "string",
   183→                            "description": "The exact memory content to forget (without the leading '- ')",
   184→                        },
   185→                    },
   186→                    "required": ["content"],
   187→                },
   188→            ),
   189→        ]
   190→
   191→    @server.call_tool()
   192→    async def call_tool(
   193→        name: str, arguments: dict
   194→    ) -> list[types.TextContent]:
   195→        if name == "execute_sql":
   196→            sql = arguments.get("sql", "")
   197→            try:
   198→                result = await db.execute_query_async(sql)
   199→                truncated_rows = result["rows"][:MAX_RESULT_ROWS]
   200→                result_json = {
   201→                    "status": "success",
   202→                    "columns": result["columns"],
   203→                    "rows": truncated_rows,
   204→                    "rowCount": result["rowCount"],
   205→                }
   206→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps(result_json, default=str))]
   207→            except Exception as e:
   208→                error_json = {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
   209→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps(error_json))]
   210→        elif name == "ask_user_question":
   211→            question_data = {
   212→                "question": arguments.get("question", ""),
   213→                "options": arguments.get("options", []),
   214→                "multi_select": arguments.get("multi_select", False),
   215→            }
   216→            question_id = pending_question_store.create(session_id, question_data)
   217→            answer = await pending_question_store.wait(session_id, question_id, timeout=300.0)
   218→            if answer is None:
   219→                result = {"timeout": True, "message": "User did not respond within the time limit."}
   220→            else:
   221→                result = answer
   222→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps(result))]
   223→        elif name == "render_chart":
   224→            data = arguments.get("data")
   225→            layout = arguments.get("layout", {})
   226→            if not isinstance(data, list):
   227→                logger.warning("render_chart called with missing data: %s", arguments)
   228→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "error", "error": "data (array of Plotly traces) is required"}))]
   229→
   230→            # Reject specs where every trace has empty data arrays.
   231→            # Data-bearing fields vary by chart type (x, y, z, values, labels, etc.).
   232→            _DATA_FIELDS = ("x", "y", "z", "values", "labels", "lat", "lon", "r", "theta",
   233→                            "lowerfence", "q1", "median", "q3", "upperfence")
   234→            has_nonempty_trace = False
   235→            for trace in data:
   236→                if not isinstance(trace, dict):
   237→                    continue
   238→                for field in _DATA_FIELDS:
   239→                    val = trace.get(field)
   240→                    if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) > 0:
   241→                        has_nonempty_trace = True
   242→                        break
   243→                if has_nonempty_trace:
   244→                    break
   245→            if not has_nonempty_trace:
   246→                logger.warning("render_chart called with all-empty data traces: %s", arguments)
   247→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   248→                    "status": "error",
   249→                    "error": "All data traces are empty — no data to chart. "
   250→                             "Check your SQL query results before calling render_chart."
   251→                }))]
   252→            # Return success with chart_spec so the orchestrator can see
   253→            # what was rendered and write coherent commentary about it.
   254→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   255→                "status": "success",
   256→                "chart_spec": {"data": data, "layout": layout},
   257→            }))]
   258→        elif name == "create_skill":
   259→            skill_name = arguments.get("name", "")
   260→            description = arguments.get("description", "")
   261→            content = arguments.get("content", "")
   262→            try:
   263→                _create_skill_file(skill_name, description, content, SKILLS_DIR)
   264→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   265→                    "success": True,
   266→                    "message": f"Skill '{skill_name}' created successfully. Users can now invoke it with /{skill_name}.",
   267→                }))]
   268→            except SkillValidationError as e:
   269→                return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   270→                    "success": False,
   271→                    "error": str(e),
   272→                }))]
   273→        elif name == "save_memory":
   274→            content = arguments.get("content", "")
   275→            category = arguments.get("category", "fact")
   276→            result = save_memory(content, category)
   277→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "success", "message": result}))]
   278→        elif name == "recall_memories":
   279→            query = arguments.get("query", "")
   280→            memories = read_memories()
   281→            if query and memories:
   282→                query_lower = query.lower()
   283→                lines = memories.split("\n")
   284→                result_lines: list[str] = []
   285→                current_header: str | None = None
   286→                section_entries: list[str] = []
   287→                for line in lines:
   288→                    if line.startswith("# "):
   289→                        # Top-level header always included
   290→                        if current_header and section_entries:
   291→                            result_lines.append(current_header)
   292→                            result_lines.append("")
   293→                            result_lines.extend(section_entries)
   294→                            result_lines.append("")
   295→                        current_header = None
   296→                        section_entries = []
   297→                        result_lines.append(line)
   298→                        result_lines.append("")
   299→                    elif line.startswith("## "):
   300→                        # Flush previous section if it had matches
   301→                        if current_header and section_entries:
   302→                            result_lines.append(current_header)
   303→                            result_lines.append("")
   304→                            result_lines.extend(section_entries)
   305→                            result_lines.append("")
   306→                        current_header = line
   307→                        section_entries = []
   308→                    elif line.startswith("- ") and query_lower in line.lower():
   309→                        section_entries.append(line)
   310→                # Flush last section
   311→                if current_header and section_entries:
   312→                    result_lines.append(current_header)
   313→                    result_lines.append("")
   314→                    result_lines.extend(section_entries)
   315→                    result_lines.append("")
   316→                memories = "\n".join(result_lines).strip()
   317→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "success", "memories": memories}))]
   318→        elif name == "forget_memory":
   319→            content = arguments.get("content", "")
   320→            result = forget_memory(content)
   321→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "success", "message": result}))]
   322→        else:
   323→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown tool: {name}")
   324→
   325→    return server
   326→
   327→
   328→async def handle_sse(request: Request) -> Response:
   329→    """Handle SSE connection. Requires session_id query param."""
   330→    session_id = request.query_params.get("session_id")
   331→    if not session_id:
   332→        return Response("session_id query parameter is required", status_code=400)
   333→
   334→    db = session_manager.get_or_create(session_id)
   335→    server = _create_mcp_server(db, session_id)
   336→
   337→    try:
   338→        async with sse_transport.connect_sse(
   339→            request.scope, request.receive, request._send
   340→        ) as streams:
   341→            await server.run(
   342→                streams[0],
   343→                streams[1],
   344→                server.create_initialization_options(),
   345→            )
   346→    except anyio.ClosedResourceError:
   347→        logger.debug("MCP SSE client disconnected (session %s)", session_id)
   348→    except BaseExceptionGroup as eg:
   349→        # The MCP library uses anyio task groups which wrap errors in
   350→        # ExceptionGroups.  Filter out ClosedResourceError (normal client
   351→        # disconnect) and re-raise anything else.
   352→        _, rest = eg.split(anyio.ClosedResourceError)
   353→        if rest:
   354→            raise rest from None
   355→        logger.debug("MCP SSE client disconnected (session %s)", session_id)
   356→
   357→    return Response()
   358→
   359→
   360→mcp_app = Starlette(
   361→    routes=[
   362→        Route("/sse", endpoint=handle_sse, methods=["GET"]),
   363→        Mount("/messages/", app=sse_transport.handle_post_message),
   364→    ]
   365→)
   366→
```

```
     1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     4→import './InlineQueryResult.css';
     5→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     6→
     7→function CopyButton({ text }: { text: string }) {
     8→  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
     9→
    10→  const handleCopy = useCallback(async () => {
    11→    try {
    12→      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    13→      setCopied(true);
    14→      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
    15→    } catch {
    16→      // fallback
    17→    }
    18→  }, [text]);
    19→
    20→  return (
    21→    <button
    22→      className="inline-query__copy-btn"
    23→      onClick={handleCopy}
    24→      title={copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy'}
    25→    >
    26→      {copied ? (
    27→        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    28→          <polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12" />
    29→        </svg>
    30→      ) : (
    31→        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    32→          <rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2" ry="2" />
    33→          <path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h9a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v1" />
    34→        </svg>
    35→      )}
    36→    </button>
    37→  );
    38→}
    39→
    40→const MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS = 20;
    41→
    42→function getToolDisplayName(result: ToolCallResult, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    43→  const name = result.toolName || '';
    44→  if (name.includes('execute_sql')) return t('sqlQuery');
    45→  if (name === 'Bash') return t('bash');
    46→  if (name === 'Read') return t('readFile');
    47→  if (name === 'Write') return t('writeFile');
    48→  if (name === 'Edit') return t('editFile');
    49→  if (name.startsWith('mcp__')) {
    50→    const parts = name.split('__');
    51→    return parts[parts.length - 1] || name;
    52→  }
    53→  return name || 'Tool';
    54→}
    55→
    56→function getToolInputDisplay(result: ToolCallResult): string | null {
    57→  if (result.sql) return result.sql;
    58→  if (result.command) return result.command;
    59→  if (result.toolInput) {
    60→    const entries = Object.entries(result.toolInput);
    61→    if (entries.length === 0) return null;
    62→    // For single-value inputs, show just the value
    63→    if (entries.length === 1) {
    64→      const val = entries[0][1];
    65→      if (val === null || val === undefined) return '';
    66→      if (typeof val === 'object') return JSON.stringify(val, null, 2);
    67→      return String(val);
    68→    }
    69→    return JSON.stringify(result.toolInput, null, 2);
    70→  }
    71→  return null;
    72→}
    73→
    74→function isSQL(result: ToolCallResult): boolean {
    75→  return !!result.sql && (result.toolName?.includes('execute_sql') ?? false);
    76→}
    77→
    78→export function InlineQueryResult({ result }: { result: ToolCallResult }) {
    79→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    80→  const displayName = getToolDisplayName(result, t);
    81→  const inputDisplay = getToolInputDisplay(result);
    82→  const isSQLTool = isSQL(result);
    83→  const hasStructuredData = result.columns.length > 0;
    84→  const displayRows = result.rows.slice(0, MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS);
    85→
    86→  // Render chart if chart_spec is present
    87→  if (result.chart_spec) {
    88→    return (
    89→      <div className="inline-query inline-query--chart">
    90→        <div className="inline-query__label inline-query__label--generic">
    91→          {(result.chart_spec?.layout?.title as string) || getToolDisplayName(result, t)}
    92→          <CopyButton text={JSON.stringify(result.chart_spec, null, 2)} />
    93→        </div>
    94→        <ChartWidget
    95→          data={result.chart_spec.data}
    96→          layout={result.chart_spec.layout}
    97→          frames={result.chart_spec.frames}
    98→        />
    99→      </div>
   100→    );
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  // Determine border color class
   104→  let variantClass = '';
   105→  if (result.error) variantClass = 'inline-query--error';
   106→  else if (result.command) variantClass = 'inline-query--bash';
   107→  else if (!isSQLTool && !result.sql) variantClass = 'inline-query--generic';
   108→
   109→  return (
   110→    <div className={`inline-query ${variantClass}`}>
   111→      {/* Tool label */}
   112→      <div className={`inline-query__label ${isSQLTool ? 'inline-query__label--sql' : result.command ? 'inline-query__label--bash' : 'inline-query__label--generic'}`}>
   113→        {displayName}
   114→      </div>
   115→
   116→      {/* Tool input */}
   117→      {inputDisplay && (
   118→        <div className="inline-query__sql">
   119→          <CopyButton text={inputDisplay} />
   120→          <code>{inputDisplay}</code>
   121→        </div>
   122→      )}
   123→
   124→      {/* Error display */}
   125→      {result.error && (
   126→        <div className="inline-query__error">{result.error}</div>
   127→      )}
   128→
   129→      {/* SQL structured results */}
   130→      {!result.error && isSQLTool && hasStructuredData && (
   131→        <>
   132→          <div className="inline-query__table-wrapper">
   133→            <table className="inline-query__table">
   134→              <thead>
   135→                <tr>
   136→                  {result.columns.map((col) => (
   137→                    <th key={col}>{col}</th>
   138→                  ))}
   139→                </tr>
   140→              </thead>
   141→              <tbody>
   142→                {displayRows.map((row, i) => (
   143→                  <tr key={i}>
   144→                    {result.columns.map((col) => (
   145→                      <td key={col}>{String(row[col] ?? '')}</td>
   146→                    ))}
   147→                  </tr>
   148→                ))}
   149→              </tbody>
   150→            </table>
   151→          </div>
   152→          <div className="inline-query__meta">
   153→            {t('rowCount', { count: result.rowCount })}
   154→            {result.rowCount > MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS && ` ${t('showingCount', { count: MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS })}`}
   155→          </div>
   156→        </>
   157→      )}
   158→
   159→      {/* Text output for non-SQL tools */}
   160→      {!result.error && result.output && (
   161→        <div className="inline-query__output">
   162→          <pre>{result.output}</pre>
   163→        </div>
   164→      )}
   165→
   166→      {/* Raw content fallback */}
   167→      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && result.rawContent && (
   168→        <div className="inline-query__raw">
   169→          <pre>{result.rawContent}</pre>
   170→        </div>
   171→      )}
   172→
   173→      {/* Pending state */}
   174→      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && !result.rawContent && inputDisplay && (
   175→        <div className="inline-query__meta">{t('executing')}</div>
   176→      )}
   177→    </div>
   178→  );
   179→}
   180→
```
