     1→import {
     2→  useCallback,
     3→  useEffect,
     4→  useRef,
     5→  useState,
     6→  type ReactNode,
     7→} from 'react';
     8→import { AgentContext } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     9→import { runAgentLoop, runAgentEditLoop } from '../agent/agentService';
    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, ToolCallResult } from '../types';
    11→import { useSessionId } from '../hooks/useSessionId';
    12→import { generateUUID } from '../utils/uuid';
    13→
    14→interface StreamState {
    15→  assistantId: string;
    16→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    17→  currentText: string;
    18→  textBuffer: string;
    19→  phase: 'thinking' | 'answer';
    20→  abortController: AbortController;
    21→  sessionId: string | null;
    22→  messages: ChatMessage[];
    23→  flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;
    24→}
    25→
    26→function generateId() {
    27→  return generateUUID();
    28→}
    29→
    30→export function AgentProvider({
    31→  children,
    32→  refreshTables,
    33→}: {
    34→  children: ReactNode;
    35→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    36→}) {
    37→  const userSessionId = useSessionId();
    38→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    39→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    40→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    41→  const pendingHistoryRef = useRef<{ role: string; content: string }[] | null>(null);
    42→  // Keep a ref in sync with messages so sendMessage always reads the latest
    43→  const messagesRef = useRef<ChatMessage[]>(messages);
    44→  useEffect(() => { messagesRef.current = messages; }, [messages]);
    45→  // Cache messages per conversation so switching away doesn't lose in-progress data
    46→  const messagesCacheRef = useRef<Map<string, ChatMessage[]>>(new Map());
    47→  // Track which conversation is currently displayed so background streams can detect they're stale
    48→  const activeConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    49→
    50→  // Per-conversation stream state map (replaces all singleton streaming refs)
    51→  const streamStatesRef = useRef<Map<string, StreamState>>(new Map());
    52→  const [streamingConversationIds, setStreamingConversationIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    53→
    54→  const flushTextForStream = useCallback((state: StreamState, conversationId: string | null) => {
    55→    const text = state.textBuffer;
    56→    if (!text) return;
    57→    state.currentText += text;
    58→    state.textBuffer = '';
    59→
    60→    // Update the stream's own messages
    61→    state.messages = state.messages.map((m) =>
    62→      m.id === state.assistantId ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    63→    );
    64→
    65→    // If this conversation is currently being viewed, update React state
    66→    if (activeConversationIdRef.current === conversationId) {
    67→      setMessages(state.messages);
    68→    }
    69→  }, []);
    70→
    71→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    72→    async (text: string, conversationId?: string | null) => {
    73→      const convId = conversationId || null;
    74→      // Per-conversation guard: don't double-send in same conversation
    75→      if (convId && streamStatesRef.current.has(convId)) return;
    76→      // Fallback guard for null-convId path (new chat before conversation created)
    77→      if (!convId && isStreaming) return;
    78→
    79→      const controller = new AbortController();
    80→      const assistantId = generateId();
    81→
    82→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    83→        id: generateId(),
    84→        role: 'user',
    85→        content: text,
    86→      };
    87→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    88→        id: assistantId,
    89→        role: 'assistant',
    90→        content: '',
    91→        toolCalls: [],
    92→        isStreaming: true,
    93→      };
    94→
    95→      // Build conversation history from current messages or cached messages
    96→      const currentMsgs = convId && activeConversationIdRef.current === convId
    97→        ? messagesRef.current
    98→        : (convId ? messagesCacheRef.current.get(convId) : messagesRef.current) || messagesRef.current;
    99→      const history = currentMsgs
   100→        .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   101→        .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   102→
   103→      const streamMessages = [...currentMsgs, userMsg, assistantMsg];
   104→
   105→      // Create per-conversation stream state
   106→      const state: StreamState = {
   107→        assistantId,
   108→        segments: [],
   109→        currentText: '',
   110→        textBuffer: '',
   111→        phase: 'thinking',
   112→        abortController: controller,
   113→        sessionId: sessionIdRef.current,
   114→        messages: streamMessages,
   115→        flushTimer: null,
   116→      };
   117→
   118→      if (convId) {
   119→        streamStatesRef.current.set(convId, state);
   120→        setStreamingConversationIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(convId));
   121→      }
   122→
   123→      // Sync the ref when sending to a newly-created conversation whose ID
   124→      // hasn't propagated through loadMessages/clearMessages yet.
   125→      if (convId && activeConversationIdRef.current !== convId) {
   126→        activeConversationIdRef.current = convId;
   127→      }
   128→
   129→      // If this is the active conversation, update React state
   130→      if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {
   131→        setMessages(streamMessages);
   132→        setIsStreaming(true);
   133→      }
   134→
   135→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session
   136→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
   137→      pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   138→      const langfuseSessionId = pendingHistory ? generateUUID() : null;
   139→
   140→      // Extract skill references
   141→      let actualMessage = text;
   142→      let skills: string[] | undefined;
   143→      const slashMatches = [...text.matchAll(/\/([a-z0-9-]+)/g)];
   144→      if (slashMatches.length > 0) {
   145→        skills = slashMatches.map((m) => m[1]);
   146→        actualMessage = slashMatches.reduce((msg, m) => msg.replace(m[0], ''), text).trim() || text;
   147→      }
   148→
   149→      // Helper to update stream messages and optionally React state
   150→      const updateMessages = (updater: (msgs: ChatMessage[]) => ChatMessage[]) => {
   151→        state.messages = updater(state.messages);
   152→        if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {
   153→          setMessages(state.messages);
   154→        }
   155→      };
   156→
   157→      // Helper to schedule text flush
   158→      const scheduleFlush = () => {
   159→        if (!state.flushTimer) {
   160→          state.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   161→            flushTextForStream(state, convId);
   162→            state.flushTimer = null;
   163→          }, 50);
   164→        }
   165→      };
   166→
   167→      // Helper to flush pending text immediately
   168→      const flushNow = () => {
   169→        if (state.flushTimer) {
   170→          clearTimeout(state.flushTimer);
   171→          state.flushTimer = null;
   172→        }
   173→        flushTextForStream(state, convId);
   174→      };
   175→
   176→      // Helper to finalize stream (called by onDone and onError)
   177→      const cleanupStream = () => {
   178→        if (state.flushTimer) {
   179→          clearTimeout(state.flushTimer);
   180→          state.flushTimer = null;
   181→        }
   182→        if (convId) {
   183→          streamStatesRef.current.delete(convId);
   184→          setStreamingConversationIds(prev => {
   185→            const next = new Set(prev);
   186→            next.delete(convId);
   187→            return next;
   188→          });
   189→        }
   190→      };
   191→
   192→      await runAgentLoop(
   193→        actualMessage,
   194→        state.sessionId,
   195→        langfuseSessionId,
   196→        pendingHistory ?? (history.length > 0 ? history : null),
   197→        conversationId,
   198→        {
   199→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   200→            state.textBuffer += chunk;
   201→            scheduleFlush();
   202→          },
   203→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   204→            flushNow();
   205→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   206→              state.segments.push({ type: 'thinking', text: state.currentText });
   207→              state.currentText = '';
   208→            }
   209→            state.phase = 'answer';
   210→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   211→              msgs.map(m =>
   212→                m.id === assistantId
   213→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...state.segments] }
   214→                  : m
   215→              )
   216→            );
   217→          },
   218→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   219→            flushNow();
   220→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   221→              const segType = state.phase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   222→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   223→              state.currentText = '';
   224→            }
   225→            state.segments.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   226→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   227→              msgs.map(m =>
   228→                m.id === assistantId
   229→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...state.segments] }
   230→                  : m
   231→              )
   232→            );
   233→          },
   234→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   235→            const pendingIdx = state.segments.findIndex(
   236→              s => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   237→            );
   238→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   239→              const pending = state.segments[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   240→              state.segments[pendingIdx] = {
   241→                type: 'tool',
   242→                toolResult: {
   243→                  ...pending,
   244→                  ...result,
   245→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   246→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   247→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   248→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   249→                },
   250→              };
   251→            } else {
   252→              state.segments.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   253→            }
   254→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   255→              msgs.map(m =>
   256→                m.id === assistantId
   257→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...state.segments] }
   258→                  : m
   259→              )
   260→            );
   261→            refreshTables();
   262→            if (result.toolName?.includes('create_skill')) {
   263→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('skills-updated'));
   264→            }
   265→            if (result.toolName?.includes('save_memory') || result.toolName?.includes('forget_memory')) {
   266→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('memories-updated'));
   267→            }
   268→          },
   269→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   270→            flushNow();
   271→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   272→              const segType = state.phase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   273→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   274→              state.currentText = '';
   275→            }
   276→            state.segments.push({
   277→              type: 'subagent_start',
   278→              subagentId: data.id,
   279→              subagentName: data.name,
   280→              text: data.prompt,
   281→            });
   282→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   283→              msgs.map(m =>
   284→                m.id === assistantId
   285→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...state.segments] }
   286→                  : m
   287→              )
   288→            );
   289→          },
   290→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   291→            state.segments.push({
   292→              type: 'subagent_end',
   293→              subagentId: data.id,
   294→              subagentName: data.name,
   295→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   296→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   297→              text: data.result,
   298→            });
   299→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   300→              msgs.map(m =>
   301→                m.id === assistantId
   302→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...state.segments] }
   303→                  : m
   304→              )
   305→            );
   306→          },
   307→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   308→            if (newSessionId) {
   309→              state.sessionId = newSessionId;
   310→              sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   311→            }
   312→            flushNow();
   313→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   314→              state.segments.push({ type: 'answer', text: state.currentText });
   315→              state.currentText = '';
   316→            }
   317→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   318→              msgs.map(m =>
   319→                m.id === assistantId
   320→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...state.segments] }
   321→                  : m
   322→              )
   323→            );
   324→            if (convId && activeConversationIdRef.current !== convId) {
   325→              messagesCacheRef.current.set(convId, state.messages);
   326→            }
   327→            cleanupStream();
   328→            if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {
   329→              setIsStreaming(false);
   330→            }
   331→          },
   332→          onError: (error) => {
   333→            flushNow();
   334→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   335→              const segType = state.phase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   336→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   337→              state.currentText = '';
   338→            }
   339→            state.segments.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   340→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   341→              msgs.map(m =>
   342→                m.id === assistantId
   343→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...state.segments] }
   344→                  : m
   345→              )
   346→            );
   347→            if (convId && activeConversationIdRef.current !== convId) {
   348→              messagesCacheRef.current.set(convId, state.messages);
   349→            }
   350→            cleanupStream();
   351→            if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {
   352→              setIsStreaming(false);
   353→            }

   380→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   381→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   382→      const convId = activeConversationIdRef.current;
   383→
   384→      // If this conversation has an active stream, abort it first
   385→      let existingMessages: ChatMessage[] | null = null;
   386→      if (convId && streamStatesRef.current.has(convId)) {
   387→        const existingState = streamStatesRef.current.get(convId)!;
   388→        existingMessages = existingState.messages;
   389→        existingState.abortController.abort();
   390→        if (existingState.flushTimer) {
   391→          clearTimeout(existingState.flushTimer);
   392→        }
   393→        streamStatesRef.current.delete(convId);
   394→        setStreamingConversationIds(prev => {
   395→          const next = new Set(prev);
   396→          if (convId) next.delete(convId);
   397→          return next;
   398→        });
   399→      }
   400→
   401→      // Truncate messages at the edit point in the backend
   402→      if (convId) {
   403→        try {
   404→          await fetch(`/api/conversations/${convId}/messages?from_sort_order=${messageIndex}`, {
   405→            method: 'DELETE',
   406→          });
   407→        } catch {
   408→          // Best-effort
   409→        }
   410→        messagesCacheRef.current.delete(convId);
   411→      }
   412→
   413→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   414→      const currentMsgs = existingMessages || messagesRef.current;
   415→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   416→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   417→        const msg = currentMsgs[i];
   418→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   419→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   420→        }
   421→      }
   422→
   423→      const controller = new AbortController();
   424→      const assistantId = generateId();
   425→
   426→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   427→        id: generateId(),
   428→        role: 'user',
   429→        content: newContent,
   430→      };
   431→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   432→        id: assistantId,
   433→        role: 'assistant',
   434→        content: '',
   435→        toolCalls: [],
   436→        isStreaming: true,
   437→      };
   438→
   439→      const truncatedMessages = currentMsgs.slice(0, messageIndex);
   440→      const streamMessages = [...truncatedMessages, userMsg, assistantMsg];
   441→
   442→      // Create per-conversation stream state
   443→      const state: StreamState = {
   444→        assistantId,
   445→        segments: [],
   446→        currentText: '',
   447→        textBuffer: '',
   448→        phase: 'thinking',
   449→        abortController: controller,
   450→        sessionId: null, // Edit starts a fresh session
   451→        messages: streamMessages,
   452→        flushTimer: null,
   453→      };
   454→
   455→      if (convId) {
   456→        streamStatesRef.current.set(convId, state);
   457→        setStreamingConversationIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(convId));
   458→      }
   459→
   460→      setMessages(streamMessages);
   461→      setIsStreaming(true);
   462→
   463→      // Helper to update stream messages and optionally React state
   464→      const updateMessages = (updater: (msgs: ChatMessage[]) => ChatMessage[]) => {
   465→        state.messages = updater(state.messages);
   466→        if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {
   467→          setMessages(state.messages);
   468→        }
   469→      };
   470→
   471→      // Helper to schedule text flush
   472→      const scheduleFlush = () => {
   473→        if (!state.flushTimer) {
   474→          state.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   475→            flushTextForStream(state, convId);
   476→            state.flushTimer = null;
   477→          }, 50);
   478→        }
   479→      };
   480→
   481→      // Helper to flush pending text immediately
   482→      const flushNow = () => {
   483→        if (state.flushTimer) {
   484→          clearTimeout(state.flushTimer);
   485→          state.flushTimer = null;
   486→        }
   487→        flushTextForStream(state, convId);
   488→      };
   489→
   490→      // Helper to finalize stream
   491→      const cleanupStream = () => {
   492→        if (state.flushTimer) {
   493→          clearTimeout(state.flushTimer);
   494→          state.flushTimer = null;
   495→        }
   496→        if (convId) {
   497→          streamStatesRef.current.delete(convId);
   498→          setStreamingConversationIds(prev => {
   499→            const next = new Set(prev);
   500→            if (convId) next.delete(convId);
   501→            return next;
   502→          });
   503→        }
   504→      };
   505→
   506→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   507→        newContent,
   508→        conversationHistory,

   512→            state.textBuffer += chunk;
   513→            scheduleFlush();
   514→          },
   515→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   516→            flushNow();
   517→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   518→              state.segments.push({ type: 'thinking', text: state.currentText });
   519→              state.currentText = '';
   520→            }
   521→            state.phase = 'answer';
   522→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   523→              msgs.map(m =>
   524→                m.id === assistantId
   525→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...state.segments] }
   526→                  : m
   527→              )
   528→            );
   529→          },
   530→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   531→            flushNow();
   532→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   533→              const segType = state.phase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   534→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   535→              state.currentText = '';
   536→            }
   537→            state.segments.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: pending });
   538→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   539→              msgs.map(m =>
   540→                m.id === assistantId
   541→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...state.segments] }
   542→                  : m
   543→              )
   544→            );
   545→          },
   546→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   547→            const pendingIdx = state.segments.findIndex(
   548→              s => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   549→            );
   550→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   551→              const pending = state.segments[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   552→              state.segments[pendingIdx] = {
   553→                type: 'tool',
   554→                toolResult: {
   555→                  ...pending,
   556→                  ...result,
   557→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   558→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   559→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   560→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   561→                },
   562→              };
   563→            } else {
   564→              state.segments.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   565→            }
   566→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   567→              msgs.map(m =>
   568→                m.id === assistantId
   569→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...state.segments] }
   570→                  : m
   571→              )
   572→            );
   573→            refreshTables();
   574→          },
   575→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {

   579→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   580→              state.currentText = '';
   581→            }
   582→            state.segments.push({
   583→              type: 'subagent_start',
   584→              subagentId: data.id,
   585→              subagentName: data.name,
   586→              text: data.prompt,
   587→            });
   588→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   589→              msgs.map(m =>
   590→                m.id === assistantId
   591→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...state.segments] }
   592→                  : m
   593→              )
   594→            );
   595→          },
   596→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {

   599→              subagentId: data.id,
   600→              subagentName: data.name,
   601→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   602→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   603→              text: data.result,
   604→            });

   607→                m.id === assistantId
   608→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...state.segments] }
   609→                  : m
   610→              )
   611→            );
   612→          },
   613→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   614→            if (newSessionId) {
   615→              state.sessionId = newSessionId;
   616→              sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   617→            }
   618→            flushNow();
   619→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {
   620→              state.segments.push({ type: 'answer', text: state.currentText });
   621→              state.currentText = '';
   622→            }
   623→            updateMessages(msgs =>
   624→              msgs.map(m =>
   625→                m.id === assistantId
   626→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...state.segments] }
   627→                  : m
   628→              )
   629→            );
   630→            if (convId && activeConversationIdRef.current !== convId) {
   631→              messagesCacheRef.current.set(convId, state.messages);

   634→            if (activeConversationIdRef.current === convId) {

   640→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {

   642→              state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
   643→              state.currentText = '';
   644→            }
   645→            state.segments.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });

   647→              msgs.map(m =>
   648→                m.id === assistantId

   650→                  : m
   651→              )
   652→            );
   653→            if (convId && activeConversationIdRef.current !== convId) {

   656→            cleanupStream();

   663→            if (state.currentText.trim()) {

   685→
   686→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   687→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   688→      const convId = activeConversationIdRef.current;
   689→      // Only block if this specific conversation is streaming
   690→      if (convId && streamStatesRef.current.has(convId)) return;
   691→
   692→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   693→      setMessages((prev) => {
   694→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   695→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   696→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   697→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   698→        return remaining;
   699→      });
   700→
   701→      // Persist the truncation to the backend
   702→      if (convId) {
   703→        fetch(`/api/conversations/${convId}/messages?from_sort_order=${messageIndex}`, {
   704→          method: 'DELETE',
   705→        }).catch(() => {
   706→          // Best-effort: if this fails the UI is already updated
   707→        });
   708→        // Invalidate cache so switching back loads from backend
   709→        messagesCacheRef.current.delete(convId);
   710→      }
   711→
   712→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   713→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   714→    },
   715→    []
   716→  );
   717→
   718→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   719→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   720→      // Find the stream state that contains this question
   721→      let targetState: StreamState | undefined;
   722→      let targetConvId: string | null = null;
   723→
   724→      // Search all active streams for the question
   725→      for (const [cid, state] of streamStatesRef.current) {
   726→        if (state.segments.some(s => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId)) {
   727→          targetState = state;
   728→          targetConvId = cid;
   729→          break;
   730→        }
   731→      }
   732→
   733→      if (targetState) {
   734→        const segIdx = targetState.segments.findIndex(
   735→          s => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   736→        );
   737→        if (segIdx !== -1) {
   738→          targetState.segments[segIdx] = {
   739→            ...targetState.segments[segIdx],
   740→            userAnswer: answers,
   741→            userFreeText: freeText,
   742→          };
   743→          targetState.messages = targetState.messages.map(m =>
   744→            m.id === targetState!.assistantId
   745→              ? { ...m, segments: [...targetState!.segments] }
   746→              : m
   747→          );
   748→          if (activeConversationIdRef.current === targetConvId) {
   749→            setMessages(targetState.messages);
   750→          }
   751→        }
   752→      }
   753→
   754→      // POST to backend
   755→      try {
   756→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   757→          method: 'POST',
   758→          headers: {
   759→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   760→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   761→          },
   762→          body: JSON.stringify({
   763→            question_id: questionId,
   764→            answers,
   765→            free_text: freeText || null,
   766→          }),
   767→        });
   768→      } catch (e) {
   769→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   770→      }
   771→    },
   772→    [userSessionId]
   773→  );
   774→
   775→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   776→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || null;
   777→    const incoming = incomingConversationId || null;
   778→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incoming;
   779→
   780→    // Save outgoing conversation's messages to cache (only if not streaming --
   781→    // streaming conversations keep their state in streamStatesRef)
   782→    if (outgoing && !streamStatesRef.current.has(outgoing)) {
   783→      const currentMsgs = messagesRef.current;
   784→      if (currentMsgs.length > 0) {
   785→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, currentMsgs);
   786→      }
   787→    }
   788→
   789→    // Check if incoming conversation has an active stream (re-attachment)
   790→    if (incoming) {
   791→      const streamState = streamStatesRef.current.get(incoming);
   792→      if (streamState) {
   793→        // Re-attach: use live stream messages
   794→        setMessages(streamState.messages);
   795→        setIsStreaming(true);
   796→        return;
   797→      }
   798→    }
   799→
   800→    // No active stream -- use backend data or cache
   801→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   802→    if (incoming) {
   803→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incoming);
   804→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length > msgs.length) {
   805→        finalMsgs = cached;
   806→      }
   807→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incoming);
   808→    }
   809→
   810→    setMessages(finalMsgs);
   811→    const hasStreamingMsg = finalMsgs.some(m => m.isStreaming);
   812→    setIsStreaming(hasStreamingMsg);
   813→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   814→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   815→  }, []);
   816→
   817→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   818→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || null;
   819→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   820→
   821→    // Save outgoing messages to cache if not streaming
   822→    if (outgoing && !streamStatesRef.current.has(outgoing)) {
   823→      const currentMsgs = messagesRef.current;
   824→      if (currentMsgs.length > 0) {
   825→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, currentMsgs);
   826→      }
   827→    }
   828→
   829→    setMessages([]);
   830→    setIsStreaming(false);
   831→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   832→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   833→  }, []);
   834→
   835→  return (
   836→    <AgentContext.Provider
   837→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion, streamingConversationIds }}
   838→    >
   839→      {children}
   840→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   841→  );
   842→}
   843→

    26→      duplicates.add(file.name);

    28→    seen.add(file.name);
    29→  }
    30→  return Array.from(duplicates);

   365→                ) : (

   367→                )}

   376→
   377→export default function App() {
   378→  const sessionId = useSessionId();
   379→  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);
   380→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   381→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   382→  const refreshTables = useCallback(async () => {
   383→    try {
   384→      const response = await fetch('/api/tables', {
   385→        headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
   386→      });
   387→      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch tables');
   388→      const data = await response.json();
   389→      setTables(data);
   390→    } catch (e) {
   391→      console.error('Failed to refresh tables:', e);
   392→    }
   393→  }, [sessionId]);
   394→
   395→  useEffect(() => {
   396→    (async () => {
   397→      try {
   398→        const response = await fetch('/api/health');
   399→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Backend is not available');
   400→        await refreshTables();
   401→      } catch (e) {
   402→        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to connect to backend');
   403→      } finally {
   404→        setLoading(false);
   405→      }
   406→    })();
   407→  }, [refreshTables]);
   408→
   409→  if (loading) {
   410→    return <div className="app-loading">Connecting to backend...</div>;
   411→  }
   412→
   413→  if (error) {
   414→    return (
   415→      <div className="app-error">Failed to connect: {error}</div>
   416→    );
   417→  }
   418→
   419→  return (
   420→    <ConfigProvider>
   421→      <LanguageProvider>
   422→        <ThemeProvider>
   423→          <ConversationProvider sessionId={sessionId}>
   424→            <AgentProvider refreshTables={refreshTables}>
   425→              <AppContent tables={tables} refreshTables={refreshTables} sessionId={sessionId} />
   426→            </AgentProvider>
   427→          </ConversationProvider>
   428→        </ThemeProvider>
   429→      </LanguageProvider>
   430→    </ConfigProvider>
   431→  );
   432→}
   433→

```
     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2→import { LanguageContext, type Language } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import en from '../i18n/en.json';
     4→import zhTW from '../i18n/zh-TW.json';
     5→
     6→const translations: Record<Language, Record<string, string>> = {
     7→  'en': en,
     8→  'zh-TW': zhTW,
     9→};
    10→
    11→function getInitialLanguage(): Language {
    12→  const stored = localStorage.getItem('language');
    13→  if (stored === 'en' || stored === 'zh-TW') return stored;
    14→  if (navigator.language.startsWith('zh')) return 'zh-TW';
    15→  return 'en';
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    19→  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(getInitialLanguage);
    20→
    21→  useEffect(() => {
    22→    document.documentElement.setAttribute('lang', language === 'zh-TW' ? 'zh-Hant' : 'en');
    23→    localStorage.setItem('language', language);
    24→  }, [language]);
    25→
    26→  const setLanguage = useCallback((lang: Language) => {
    27→    setLanguageState(lang);
    28→  }, []);
    29→
    30→  const t = useCallback((key: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>): string => {
    31→    let value = translations[language][key] ?? key;
    32→    if (params) {
    33→      for (const [paramKey, paramValue] of Object.entries(params)) {
    34→        value = value.replace(new RegExp(`\\{${paramKey}\\}`, 'g'), String(paramValue));
    35→      }
    36→    }
    37→    return value;
    38→  }, [language]);
    39→
    40→  return (
    41→    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, t }}>
    42→      {children}
    43→    </LanguageContext.Provider>
    44→  );
    45→}
    46→
```

     1→import { useState, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2→import { ThemeContext } from '../hooks/useTheme';
     3→
     4→type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
     5→
     6→function getInitialTheme(): Theme {
     7→  const stored = localStorage.getItem('theme');
     8→  if (stored === 'dark' || stored === 'light') return stored;
     9→  if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches) return 'dark';
    10→  return 'light';
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    14→  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(getInitialTheme);
    15→
    16→  useEffect(() => {
    17→    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
    18→    localStorage.setItem('theme', theme);
    19→  }, [theme]);
    20→
    21→  const toggleTheme = () => setTheme((prev) => (prev === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
    22→
    23→  return (
    24→    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme }}>
    25→      {children}
    26→    </ThemeContext.Provider>
    27→  );
    28→}
    29→

`frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`:\n\n```tsx\nimport { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport embed from 'vega-embed';\nimport { useTheme } from '../hooks/useTheme';\nimport { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';\n\ninterface VegaLiteChartWidgetProps {\n  spec: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nexport function VegaLiteChartWidget({ spec }: VegaLiteChartWidgetProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const { theme } = useTheme();\n  const isDark = theme === 'dark';\n  const { t } = useTranslation();\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;\n\n    const darkConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      axis: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        gridColor: '#374151',\n        domainColor: '#374151',\n      },\n      legend: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      title: {\n        color: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const lightConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const fullSpec = {\n      ...spec,\n      width: 'container',\n      autosize: { type: 'fit', contains: 'padding' },\n      config: isDark ? darkConfig : lightConfig,\n    };\n\n    let disposed = false;\n    embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {\n      actions: true,\n      renderer: 'svg',\n    })\n      .then((result) => {\n        if (disposed) {\n          result.finalize();\n        }\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        if (!disposed) {\n          setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');\n        }\n      });\n\n    return () => {\n      disposed = true;\n    };\n  }, [spec, isDark]);\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div style={{ padding: '1rem', textAlign: 'center', opacity: 0.6 }}>\n        {t('chartRenderError')}: {error}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: 400 }} />;\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3: Add i18n key for error**\n\nIn `frontend/src/i18n/en.json`, add:\n```json\n\"chartRenderError\": \"Chart rendering failed\"\n```\n\nIn      1→# Chart Library Switch Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Add a global Plotly/Vega-Lite toggle in the agent panel header so users can choose which charting library renders visualizations.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Frontend stores library preference in localStorage via a React context. The preference is sent with every chat request. Backend dynamically adjusts the agent's system prompt and render_chart tool schema. Frontend dispatches chart_spec to either Plotly or Vega-Lite renderer based on a `library` field.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** React, TypeScript, vega-embed, FastAPI/Pydantic, Claude Agent SDK

    13→### Task 1: Add ChartLibrary context and hook
    14→
    15→**Files:**
    16→- Create: `frontend/src/hooks/useChartLibrary.ts`
    17→- Create: `frontend/src/contexts/ChartLibraryContext.tsx`
    18→
    19→**Step 1: Create the hook file**
    20→
    21→Create `frontend/src/hooks/useChartLibrary.ts`:
    22→
    23→```typescript
    24→import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
    25→
    26→export type ChartLibrary = 'plotly' | 'vegalite';
    27→
    28→interface ChartLibraryContextValue {
    29→  chartLibrary: ChartLibrary;
    30→  setChartLibrary: (lib: ChartLibrary) => void;
    31→}
    32→
    33→export const ChartLibraryContext = createContext<ChartLibraryContextValue>({
    34→  chartLibrary: 'plotly',
    35→  setChartLibrary: () => {},
    36→});
    37→
    38→export function useChartLibrary() {
    39→  return useContext(ChartLibraryContext);
    40→}
    41→```
    42→
    43→**Step 2: Create the provider**
    44→
    45→Create `frontend/src/contexts/ChartLibraryContext.tsx`:
    46→
    47→```typescript
    48→import { useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';
    49→import { ChartLibraryContext, type ChartLibrary } from '../hooks/useChartLibrary';
    50→
    51→function getInitialLibrary(): ChartLibrary {
    52→  const stored = localStorage.getItem('chartLibrary');
    53→  if (stored === 'plotly' || stored === 'vegalite') return stored;
    54→  return 'plotly';
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function ChartLibraryProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    58→  const [chartLibrary, setChartLibraryState] = useState<ChartLibrary>(getInitialLibrary);
    59→
    60→  const setChartLibrary = useCallback((lib: ChartLibrary) => {
    61→    setChartLibraryState(lib);
    62→    localStorage.setItem('chartLibrary', lib);
    63→  }, []);
    64→
    65→  return (
    66→    <ChartLibraryContext.Provider value={{ chartLibrary, setChartLibrary }}>
    67→      {children}
    68→    </ChartLibraryContext.Provider>
    69→  );
    70→}
    71→```
    72→

   366→Replace the charting workflow section (lines 53-74) with a conditional:

   368→For Plotly (current text, no change).

   370→For Vega-Lite:
   371→```python
   372→VEGALITE_CHARTING_WORKFLOW = """Charting workflow (follow this exactly):
   373→1. Run execute_sql to get the data you need for a chart.
   374→2. Call render_chart with TWO required parameters:
   375→   - `library`: "vegalite"
   376→   - `spec`: a Vega-Lite specification object containing:
   377→     - `$schema`: "https://vega.github.io/schema/vega-lite/v5.json"
   378→     - `title`: a descriptive chart title (required)
   379→     - `mark`: the mark type (e.g. "bar", "line", "point", "arc", "boxplot", "area", "rect")
   380→     - `encoding`: channel encodings (x, y, color, size, etc.) referencing field names
   381→     - `data`: {"values": [...]} with the actual data rows
   382→   Both `library` and `spec` are required — the tool WILL accept both. Do not second-guess this.
   383→3. After the chart renders, write your narrative text discussing what the chart shows.
   384→4. Repeat steps 1-3 for each additional chart. This produces interleaved charts and narrative.
   385→- Do NOT render all charts first and then write all narrative at the end.
   386→- Do NOT output chart JSON as a code block. Always use the render_chart tool.
   387→- NEVER include Vega-Lite JSON, spec objects, or encoding details in your final answer text unless the user explicitly asks to see the raw JSON. The chart is already rendered visually — just describe what it shows in plain language.
   388→
   389→Charting guidelines:
   390→- Choose the most appropriate mark type (bar, line, point, arc, boxplot, area, rect, etc.).
   391→- For pie charts, use mark "arc" with theta and color encodings.
   392→- For multi-series data, use color encoding to distinguish series.
   393→- Keep the chart clean and readable.
   394→- IMPORTANT — keep data small. Pre-aggregate in SQL instead of passing raw rows:
   395→  - Box plots: compute summary stats in SQL and pass as values. Use mark "boxplot".
   396→  - Histograms: compute bin counts in SQL, then render as bar chart with the bin edges.
   397→  - Scatter / line with many rows: sample (ORDER BY random() LIMIT 200) or aggregate so data has at most ~200 points.
   398→  - General rule: data.values should have at most ~200 rows.
   399→"""
   400→```
   401→
   402→Use `chart_library` parameter to select which workflow text to include.

   462→
   463→In `backend/app/mcp_sse.py`, update the `render_chart` handler (starting at line 223):
   464→
   465→```python
   466→elif name == "render_chart":
   467→    library = arguments.get("library", "plotly")
   468→
   469→    if library == "vegalite":
   470→        spec = arguments.get("spec")
   471→        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
   472→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   473→                "status": "error",
   474→                "error": "spec (Vega-Lite specification object) is required when library is 'vegalite'"
   475→            }))]
   476→        # Check spec has data
   477→        data_values = spec.get("data", {}).get("values", [])
   478→        if not data_values:
   479→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   480→                "status": "error",
   481→                "error": "Vega-Lite spec must contain data.values with at least one row."
   482→            }))]
   483→        return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   484→            "status": "success",
   485→            "chart_spec": {"library": "vegalite", "spec": spec},
   486→        }))]
   487→    else:
   488→        # Plotly path (existing logic)
   489→        data = arguments.get("data")
   490→        layout = arguments.get("layout", {})
   491→        if not isinstance(data, list):
   492→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({"status": "error", "error": "data (array of Plotly traces) is required"}))]
   493→
   494→        _DATA_FIELDS = ("x", "y", "z", "values", "labels", "lat", "lon", "r", "theta",
   495→                        "lowerfence", "q1", "median", "q3", "upperfence")
   496→        has_nonempty_trace = False
   497→        for trace in data:
   498→            if not isinstance(trace, dict):
   499→                continue
   500→            for field in _DATA_FIELDS:
   501→                val = trace.get(field)
   502→                if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) > 0:
   503→                    has_nonempty_trace = True
   504→                    break
   505→            if has_nonempty_trace:
   506→                break
   507→        if not has_nonempty_trace:
   508→            return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   509→                "status": "error",
   510→                "error": "All data traces are empty — no data to chart. "
   511→                         "Check your SQL query results before calling render_chart."
   512→            }))]
   513→        return [types.TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps({
   514→            "status": "success",
   515→            "chart_spec": {"library": "plotly", "data": data, "layout": layout},
   516→        }))]

Since the MCP handler now returns a `chart_spec` with `library` included in the JSON response, and the frontend also reads `result_data["chart_spec"]` from the SSE, both paths should work.

Since the MCP result now includes `library`, the frontend will receive it.    536→### Task 7: Frontend — install vega dependencies and create VegaLiteChartWidget
   537→
   538→**Files:**
   539→- Modify: `frontend/package.json` (via npm install)
   540→- Create: `frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`
   541→
   542→**Step 1: Install vega dependencies**
   543→
   544→```bash
   545→cd frontend && npm install vega vega-lite vega-embed
   546→```
   547→
   548→**Step 2: Create VegaLiteChartWidget**
   549→
   550→Create `frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`:
   551→
   552→```tsx
   553→import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
   554→import embed from 'vega-embed';
   555→import { useTheme } from '../hooks/useTheme';
   556→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
   557→
   558→interface VegaLiteChartWidgetProps {
   559→  spec: Record<string, unknown>;
   560→}
   561→
   562→export function VegaLiteChartWidget({ spec }: VegaLiteChartWidgetProps) {
   563→  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   564→  const { theme } = useTheme();
   565→  const isDark = theme === 'dark';
   566→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   567→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   568→
   569→  useEffect(() => {
   570→    if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;
   571→
   572→    const darkConfig = {
   573→      background: 'transparent',
   574→      axis: {
   575→        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',
   576→        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',
   577→        gridColor: '#374151',
   578→        domainColor: '#374151',
   579→      },
   580→      legend: {
   581→        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',
   582→        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',
   583→      },
   584→      title: {
   585→        color: '#e2e8f0',
   586→      },
   587→      view: {
   588→        stroke: 'transparent',
   589→      },
   590→    };
   591→
   592→    const lightConfig = {
   593→      background: 'transparent',
   594→      view: {
   595→        stroke: 'transparent',
   596→      },
   597→    };
   598→
   599→    const fullSpec = {
   600→      ...spec,
   601→      width: 'container',
   602→      autosize: { type: 'fit', contains: 'padding' },
   603→      config: isDark ? darkConfig : lightConfig,
   604→    };
   605→
   606→    let disposed = false;
   607→    embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {
   608→      actions: true,
   609→      renderer: 'svg',
   610→    })
   611→      .then((result) => {
   612→        if (disposed) {
   613→          result.finalize();
   614→        }
   615→      })
   616→      .catch((err) => {
   617→        if (!disposed) {
   618→          setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');
   619→        }
   620→      });
   621→
   622→    return () => {
   623→      disposed = true;
   624→    };
   625→  }, [spec, isDark]);
   626→
   627→  if (error) {
   628→    return (
   629→      <div style={{ padding: '1rem', textAlign: 'center', opacity: 0.6 }}>
   630→        {t('chartRenderError')}: {error}
   631→      </div>
   632→    );
   633→  }
   634→
   635→  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: 400 }} />;
   636→}
   637→```
   638→
   639→**Step 3: Add i18n key for error**

   642→```json
   643→"chartRenderError": "Chart rendering failed"
   644→```
   645→
   646→In `frontend/src/i18n/zh-TW.json`, add:
   647→```json
   648→"chartRenderError": "圖表渲染失敗"
   649→```
   650→

{
  "description": "Implement Task 1: ChartLibrary context",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 1: Add ChartLibrary context and hook\n\n## Task Description\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `frontend/src/hooks/useChartLibrary.ts`\n- Create: `frontend/src/contexts/ChartLibraryContext.tsx`\n- Modify: `frontend/src/App.tsx`\n\n**Step 1: Create the hook file**\n\nCreate `frontend/src/hooks/useChartLibrary.ts`:\n\n```typescript\nimport { createContext, useContext } from 'react';\n\nexport type ChartLibrary = 'plotly' | 'vegalite';\n\ninterface ChartLibraryContextValue {\n  chartLibrary: ChartLibrary;\n  setChartLibrary: (lib: ChartLibrary) => void;\n}\n\nexport const ChartLibraryContext = createContext<ChartLibraryContextValue>({\n  chartLibrary: 'plotly',\n  setChartLibrary: () => {},\n});\n\nexport function useChartLibrary() {\n  return useContext(ChartLibraryContext);\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Create the provider**\n\nCreate `frontend/src/contexts/ChartLibraryContext.tsx`:\n\n```typescript\nimport { useState, useCallback, type ReactNode } from 'react';\nimport { ChartLibraryContext, type ChartLibrary } from '../hooks/useChartLibrary';\n\nfunction getInitialLibrary(): ChartLibrary {\n  const stored = localStorage.getItem('chartLibrary');\n  if (stored === 'plotly' || stored === 'vegalite') return stored;\n  return 'plotly';\n}\n\nexport function ChartLibraryProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [chartLibrary, setChartLibraryState] = useState<ChartLibrary>(getInitialLibrary);\n\n  const setChartLibrary = useCallback((lib: ChartLibrary) => {\n For Plotly: pass `data` (array of traces) and `layout` (with title). \"\n        \"For Vega-Lite: pass `library` as \\\"vegalite\\\" and `spec` (full Vega-Lite spec with title).\"\n    ),\n    inputSchema={\n        \"type\": \"object\",\n        \"properties\": {\n            \"library\": {\n                \"type\": \"string\",\n                \"enum\": [\"plotly\", \"vegalite\"],\n                \"default\": \"plotly\",\n                \"description\": \"Chart library to use: 'plotly' (default) or 'vegalite'\",\n            },\n            \"data\": {\n                \"type\": \"array\",\n                \"description\": \"Array of Plotly trace objects (for Plotly mode)\",\n                \"items\": {\"type\": \"object\"},\n            },\n            \"layout\": {\n                \"type\": \"object\",\n                \"description\": \"Plotly layout object (for Plotly mode)\",\n                \"properties\": {\n                    \"title\": {\"type\": \"string\"},\n                },\n                \"required\": [\"title\"],\n            },\n            \"spec\": {\n                \"type\": \"object\",\n                \"description\": \"Full Vega-Lite specification (for Vega-Lite mode)\",\n            },\n        },\n        \"required\": [],\n    },\n),\n```\n\n**Step 2: Update render_chart handler**\n\nFind the render_chart handler (currently starting at `elif name == \"render_chart\":` around line 223) and replace the entire block with:\n\n```python\nelif name == \"render_chart\":\n    library = arguments.get(\"library\", \"plotly\")\n\n    if library == \"vegalite\":\n        spec = arguments.get(\"spec\")\n        if not isinstance(spec, dict):\n            return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\n                \"status\": \"error\",\n                \"error\": \"spec (Vega-Lite specification object) is required when library is 'vegalite'\"\n            }))]\n        # Check spec has data\n        data_obj = spec.get(\"data\", {})\n        data_values = data_obj.get(\"values\", []) if isinstance(data_obj, dict) else []\n        if not data_values:\n            return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\n                \"status\": \"error\",\n                \"error\": \"Vega-Lite spec must contain data.values with at least one row.\"\n            }))]\n        return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"chart_spec\": {\"library\": \"vegalite\", \"spec\": spec},\n        }))]\n    else:\n        # Plotly path (existing logic)\n        data = arguments.get(\"data\")\n        layout = arguments.get(\"layout\", {})\n        if not isinstance(data, list):\n            logger.warning(\"render_chart called with missing data: %s\", arguments)\n            return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\"status\": \"error\", \"error\": \"data (array of Plotly traces) is required\"}))]\n\n        _DATA_FIELDS = (\"x\", \"y\", \"z\", \"values\", \"labels\", \"lat\", \"lon\", \"r\", \"theta\",\n                        \"lowerfence\", \"q1\", \"median\", \"q3\", \"upperfence\")\n        has_nonempty_trace = False\n        for trace in data:\n            if not isinstance(trace, dict):\n                continue\n            for field in _DATA_FIELDS:\n                val = trace.get(field)\n                if isinstance(val, list) and len(val) > 0:\n                    has_nonempty_trace = True\n                    break\n            if has_nonempty_trace:\n                break\n        if not has_nonempty_trace:\n            logger.warning(\"render_chart called with all-empty data traces: %s\", arguments)\n            return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\n                \"status\": \"error\",\n                \"error\": \"All data traces are empty — no data to chart. \"\n                         \"Check your SQL query results before calling render_chart.\"\n            }))]\n        return [types.TextContent(type=\"text\", text=json.dumps({\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"chart_spec\": {\"library\": \"plotly\", \"data\": data, \"layout\": layout},\n        }))]\n```\n\nIMPORTANT: The Plotly path now includes `\"library\": \"plotly\"` in the chart_spec response. {
  "description": "Implement Task 7: VegaLite widget",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 7: Install vega deps and create VegaLiteChartWidget\n\n## Task Description\n\n**Step 1: Install vega dependencies**\n\n```bash\ncd /Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend && npm install vega vega-lite vega-embed\n```\n\n**Step 2: Create VegaLiteChartWidget**\n\nCreate `frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`:\n\n```tsx\nimport { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport embed from 'vega-embed';\nimport { useTheme } from '../hooks/useTheme';\nimport { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';\n\ninterface VegaLiteChartWidgetProps {\n  spec: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nexport function VegaLiteChartWidget({ spec }: VegaLiteChartWidgetProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const { theme } = useTheme();\n  const isDark = theme === 'dark';\n  const { t } = useTranslation();\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;\n\n    const darkConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      axis: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        gridColor: '#374151',\n        domainColor: '#374151',\n      },\n      legend: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      title: {\n        color: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const lightConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const fullSpec = {\n      ...spec,\n      width: 'container',\n      autosize: { type: 'fit', contains: 'padding' },\n      config: isDark ? darkConfig : lightConfig,\n    };\n\n    let disposed = false;\n    embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {\n      actions: true,\n      renderer: 'svg',\n    })\n      .then((result) => {\n        if (disposed) {\n          result.finalize();\n        }\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        if (!disposed) {\n          setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');\n        }\n      });\n\n    return () => {\n      disposed = true;\n    };\n  }, [spec, isDark]);\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div style={{ padding: '1rem', textAlign: 'center', opacity: 0.6 }}>\n        {t('chartRenderError')}: {error}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: 400 }} />;\n}\n```\n\n**Step 3: Add i18n key for error**\n\nIn `frontend/src/i18n/en.json`, add:\n```json\n\"chartRenderError\": \"Chart rendering failed\"\n```\n\nIn `frontend/src/i18n/zh-TW.json`, add:\n```json\n\"chartRenderError\": \"圖表渲染失敗\"\n```\n\n**Step 4: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx frontend/package.json frontend/package-lock.json frontend/src/i18n/en.json frontend/src/i18n/zh-TW.json\ngit commit -m \"feat: add VegaLiteChartWidget with vega-embed renderer\"\n```\n\n## Context\n\nThe existing ChartWidget.tsx uses react-plotly.js to render Plotly charts. This new VegaLiteChartWidget uses vega-embed to render Vega-Lite specs. It follows the same theme-aware pattern (dark/light mode colors). The vega-embed library is the standard way to embed Vega-Lite charts.\n\nThe useTheme hook is at `frontend/src/hooks/useTheme.ts` and provides `{ theme, toggleTheme }`. The useTranslation hook is at `frontend/src/hooks/useTranslation.ts`.\n\nWork from: /Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch",

Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me compile the review.

---

## Code Review: Chart Library Switch Feature

### Overall Assessment

The implementation is well-structured, closely follows both the design doc and implementation plan, and the TypeScript compiles cleanly. The commit history is clean and logical (10 commits, each focused on one concern). The feature covers the full stack: context/hook, UI toggle, types, API plumbing, backend prompt/tool changes, new renderer, and routing logic.

Below are findings categorized by severity.

---

### Critical Issues (Must Fix)

**1. VegaLiteChartWidget does not finalize vega-embed on re-render or unmount (memory/DOM leak)**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`, lines 54-72

```typescript
let disposed = false;
embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {
  actions: true,
  renderer: 'svg',
})
  .then((result) => {
    if (disposed) {
      result.finalize();
    }
  })
  .catch((err) => {
    if (!disposed) {
      setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');
    }
  });

return () => {
  disposed = true;
};
```

The problem: when `disposed` is `false` at the time `.then()` fires (the normal, happy-path case), the `result` reference is never stored. On the next effect run (e.g., when `isDark` changes and the theme toggles), or when the component unmounts, there is no way to call `result.finalize()`. This means:
- Each theme toggle creates a new vega-embed instance without destroying the previous one.
- On component unmount, the vega view is never cleaned up.

The fix is to store the result in a ref and finalize in the cleanup:

```typescript
const viewRef = useRef<{ finalize: () => void } | null>(null);

useEffect(() => {
  if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;

  // Clean up previous view before creating a new one
  viewRef.current?.finalize();
  viewRef.current = null;

  // ... config setup ...

  let cancelled = false;
  embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {
    actions: true,
    renderer: 'svg',
  })
    .then((result) => {
      if (cancelled) {
        result.finalize();
      } else {
        viewRef.current = result;
      }
    })
    .catch((err) => {
      if (!cancelled) {
        setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');
      }
    });

  return () => {
    cancelled = true;
    viewRef.current?.finalize();
    viewRef.current = null;
  };
}, [spec, isDark]);
```

---

### Important Issues (Should Fix)

**2. `data` is required (non-optional) in the `chart_spec` TypeScript type, but Vega-Lite SSE payloads do not include it**

Files:
- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend/src/types.ts`, line 34 and line 68
- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`, line 225

In `types.ts`, both `ToolCallResult.chart_spec` and `ContentSegment.chart_spec` define `data` as a required field:

```typescript
chart_spec?: {
  library?: 'plotly' | 'vegalite';
  data: unknown[];           // <-- required, not optional
  layout?: Record<string, unknown>;
  frames?: unknown[];
  spec?: Record<string, unknown>;
};
```

However, the backend Vega-Lite response is `{"library": "vegalite", "spec": {...}}` -- no `data` field at all. The SSE parser compensates with a fallback (`?? []`) at line 225 of `agentService.ts`:

```typescript
data: ((data.chart_spec as Record<string, unknown>).data as unknown[]) ?? [],
```

This works at runtime but is semantically wrong. For Vega-Lite charts, `data` is always an empty array, which is misleading and could cause bugs if any downstream code checks `data.length` to determine if a chart has data. The `data` field should be optional:

```typescript
data?: unknown[];
```

Making this change would also require updating a few places that reference `chart_spec.data` without an optional check, but this is the correct modeling.

**3. Error state in VegaLiteChartWidget is not reset on spec change**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend/src/components/VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx`

If a chart fails to render and sets `error`, then the `spec` prop changes (e.g., a new chart), the error state persists and the error UI is shown instead of attempting to render the new spec. The `useEffect` never clears `error` before re-embedding. Add `setError(null)` at the top of the effect:

```typescript
useEffect(() => {
  if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;
  setError(null);  // Reset error on new render attempt
  // ...
```

---

### Suggestions (Nice to Have)

**4. Backend does not validate `chart_library` value**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/backend/app/routes/chat.py`, lines 20 and 28

The `chart_library` field is typed as `str` with default `"plotly"`. There is no validation that it must be one of `"plotly"` or `"vegalite"`. A typo from the frontend would silently fall through to the Plotly code path (because of the `else` branch), which is arguably safe behavior, but using a Pydantic `Literal` would be more explicit:

```python
from typing import Literal
chart_library: Literal["plotly", "vegalite"] = "plotly"
```

**5. System prompt switched from a plain string to an f-string, but existing curly braces in the Plotly charting instructions are not escaped**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/backend/app/agent.py`

The main system prompt was changed from a regular string to an f-string (line 89: `prompt = f"""You are a helpful...`), and the charting workflow strings contain curly braces like `{"title": "My Chart"}`. However, these are inside the `charting_workflow` variable which is assigned as a regular (non-f-string) triple-quoted string, and then interpolated into the f-string via `{charting_workflow}`. So the curly braces in the charting instructions are plain text by the time they are interpolated, meaning there is **no actual bug here**. This is a note for future maintainers: if those charting workflow strings were ever inlined into the f-string directly, the curly braces would need escaping with `{{` and `}}`.

**6. The `agent-panel__actions` CSS changed from `grid` to `flex`, which may affect the Export/Clear button layout**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.worktrees/chart-library-switch/frontend/src/components/AgentPanel.css`

The original CSS was:
```css
.agent-panel__actions {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 8px;
  width: 210px;
}
```

It is now:
```css
.agent-panel__actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}
```

The fixed `width: 210px` and equal-width columns for Export/Clear are gone. This is likely fine (and possibly intentional to accommodate the toggle), but worth verifying visually that the buttons don't look misaligned with different i18n strings, since the previous grid layout ensured equal button widths.

---

### Spec Compliance Summary

| Design Doc Requirement | Status |
|---|---|
| Global toggle in AgentPanel header (left of Export) | Implemented correctly |
| localStorage persistence with key `chartLibrary` | Implemented correctly |
| `ChartLibraryContext` React context | Implemented correctly |
| `chart_spec.library` field in types | Implemented correctly |
| New `VegaLiteChartWidget` with vega-embed | Implemented correctly (with cleanup bug noted above) |
| `InlineQueryResult` routes by library | Implemented correctly |
| `MessageBubble` routes by library | Implemented correctly |
| `agentService` sends `chart_library` in POST | Implemented correctly |
| `agentService` parses `library` and `spec` from SSE | Implemented correctly |
| Backend `ChatRequest.chart_library` | Implemented correctly |
| Backend `build_system_prompt` dual-library | Implemented correctly |
| Backend `render_chart` MCP tool dual-library | Implemented correctly |
| SSE event tagging with `library` | Implemented correctly |
| vega, vega-lite, vega-embed dependencies | Installed |
| i18n keys for both languages | Added |
| Default Plotly, backward compat | Maintained |
| Switching only affects new charts | Correct by design (library comes from SSE, not current toggle state) |

### Files Listed in Design Doc vs Actually Modified

All 12 files listed in the design doc's "Files to Modify" table were modified. The plan also listed `useAgent.ts` as a file to modify, but that was unnecessary -- the `chartLibrary` is consumed in `AgentContext.tsx` directly, which is the correct location since it is where `runAgentLoop` and `runAgentEditLoop` are called.

### What Was Done Well

- Clean separation of concerns: hook, context, provider, widget are all in their own files.
- Backward compatibility is preserved: missing `library` field defaults to Plotly everywhere.
- The charting workflow system prompt for Vega-Lite is thorough with practical guidelines (pre-aggregation, data size limits, mark types).
- Both the `tool_result` and `subagent_end` SSE code paths were updated for Vega-Lite, covering both rendering paths.
- The backend Vega-Lite validation checks for `data.values` existence, which is important since Vega-Lite specs without data would render nothing.

### Summary of Action Items

1. **Critical**: Fix VegaLiteChartWidget to store and finalize the vega-embed result on cleanup/re-render. Without this, theme toggles and unmounts leak vega views.
2. **Important**: Make `data` optional in the `chart_spec` type definition, since Vega-Lite specs do not include it.
3. **Important**: Reset error state in VegaLiteChartWidget when spec changes.
4. **Suggestion**: Use `Literal["plotly", "vegalite"]` for the Pydantic model field.

    24→        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',
    25→        gridColor: '#374151',
    26→        domainColor: '#374151',
    27→      },
    28→      legend: {
    29→        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',
    30→        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',
    31→      },
    32→      title: {
    33→        color: '#e2e8f0',
    34→      },
    35→      view: {
    36→        stroke: 'transparent',
    37→      },
    38→    };
    39→
    40→    const lightConfig = {
    41→      background: 'transparent',
    42→      view: {
    43→        stroke: 'transparent',
    44→      },
    45→    };
    46→
    47→    const fullSpec = {
    48→      ...spec,
    49→      width: 'container',
    50→      autosize: { type: 'fit', contains: 'padding' },
    51→      config: isDark ? darkConfig : lightConfig,
    52→    };
    53→
    54→    let disposed = false;
    55→    embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {
    56→      actions: true,
    57→      renderer: 'svg',
    58→    })
    59→      .then((result) => {
    60→        if (disposed) {
    61→          result.finalize();
    62→        }
    63→      })
    64→      .catch((err) => {
    65→        if (!disposed) {
    66→          setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');
    67→        }
    68→      });
    69→
    70→    return () => {
    71→      disposed = true;
    72→    };
    73→  }, [spec, isDark]);
    74→
    75→  if (error) {
    76→    return (
    77→      <div style={{ padding: '1rem', textAlign: 'center', opacity: 0.6 }}>
    78→        {t('chartRenderError')}: {error}
    79→      </div>
    80→    );
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: 400 }} />;
    84→}
    85→

     1→from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header
     2→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, StreamingResponse
     3→from pydantic import BaseModel
     4→
     5→from app.agent import stream_chat
     6→from app.database import Database
     7→from app.dependencies import get_session_db
     8→from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store
     9→
    10→router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["chat"])
    11→
    12→
    13→class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    14→    message: str
    15→    session_id: str | None = None
    16→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    17→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    18→    skills: list[str] | None = None
    19→    conversation_id: str | None = None
    20→    chart_library: str = "plotly"
    21→
    22→
    23→class ChatEditRequest(BaseModel):
    24→    new_message: str
    25→    conversation_history: list[dict] = []
    26→    langfuse_session_id: str | None = None
    27→    conversation_id: str | None = None
    28→    chart_library: str = "plotly"
    29→
    30→
    31→class QuestionResponseRequest(BaseModel):
    32→    question_id: str
    33→    answers: list[str] = []
    34→    free_text: str | None = None
    35→
    36→
    37→@router.post("/chat")
    38→async def chat(
    39→    request: ChatRequest,
    40→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    41→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    42→):
    43→    return StreamingResponse(
    44→        stream_chat(
    45→            request.message,
    46→            request.session_id,
    47→            db,
    48→            conversation_history=request.conversation_history or None,
    49→            langfuse_session_id=request.langfuse_session_id,
    50→            backend_session_id=x_session_id,
    51→            skills=request.skills,
    52→            conversation_id=request.conversation_id,
    53→            chart_library=request.chart_library,
    54→        ),
    55→        media_type="text/event-stream",
    56→        headers={
    57→            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    58→            "Connection": "keep-alive",
    59→            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    60→        },
    61→    )
    62→
    63→
    64→@router.post("/chat/edit")
    65→async def chat_edit(
    66→    request: ChatEditRequest,
    67→    db: Database = Depends(get_session_db),
    68→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    69→):
    70→    """Edit a message: start a fresh session with conversation history as context."""
    71→    return StreamingResponse(
    72→        stream_chat(
    73→            request.new_message,
    74→            session_id=None,
    75→            db=db,
    76→            conversation_history=request.conversation_history,
    77→            langfuse_session_id=request.langfuse_session_id,
    78→            backend_session_id=x_session_id,
    79→            conversation_id=request.conversation_id,
    80→            chart_library=request.chart_library,
    81→        ),
    82→        media_type="text/event-stream",
    83→        headers={
    84→            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    85→            "Connection": "keep-alive",
    86→            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
    87→        },
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→@router.post("/chat/respond")
    92→async def respond_to_question(
    93→    request: QuestionResponseRequest,
    94→    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
    95→):
    96→    """Respond to a pending user question from the agent."""
    97→    pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(x_session_id)
    98→    if pending is None or pending["question_id"] != request.question_id:
    99→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "No pending question found"})
   100→
   101→    answer = {"answers": request.answers}
   102→    if request.free_text:
   103→        answer["free_text"] = request.free_text
   104→    pending_question_store.respond(x_session_id, request.question_id, answer)
   105→    return {"status": "ok"}
   106→

  "new_string": "export function VegaLiteChartWidget({ spec }: VegaLiteChartWidgetProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const viewRef = useRef<{ finalize: () => void } | null>(null);\n  const { theme } = useTheme();\n  const isDark = theme === 'dark';\n  const { t } = useTranslation();\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!containerRef.current || !spec) return;\n\n    setError(null);\n    viewRef.current?.finalize();\n    viewRef.current = null;\n\n    const darkConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      axis: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        gridColor: '#374151',\n        domainColor: '#374151',\n      },\n      legend: {\n        labelColor: '#e2e8f0',\n        titleColor: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      title: {\n        color: '#e2e8f0',\n      },\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const lightConfig = {\n      background: 'transparent',\n      view: {\n        stroke: 'transparent',\n      },\n    };\n\n    const fullSpec = {\n      ...spec,\n      width: 'container',\n      autosize: { type: 'fit', contains: 'padding' },\n      config: isDark ? darkConfig : lightConfig,\n    };\n\n    let cancelled = false;\n    embed(containerRef.current, fullSpec as never, {\n      actions: true,\n      renderer: 'svg',\n    })\n      .then((result) => {\n        if (cancelled) {\n          result.finalize();\n        } else {\n          viewRef.current = result;\n        }\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        if (!cancelled) {\n          setError(err?.message || 'Failed to render Vega-Lite chart');\n        }\n      });\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n      viewRef.current?.finalize();\n      viewRef.current = null;\n    };\n  }, [spec, isDark]);"
}

Implementation complete. Here's a summary of what was built across **11 commits** on `feature/chart-library-switch`:

**Frontend (7 files created/modified):**
- `useChartLibrary.ts` + `ChartLibraryContext.tsx` — React context with localStorage persistence
- `AgentPanel.tsx` + `.css` — Segmented Plotly/Vega-Lite toggle left of Export button
- `VegaLiteChartWidget.tsx` — New renderer using vega-embed with dark/light theme support
- `InlineQueryResult.tsx` + `MessageBubble.tsx` — Route chart_spec to correct renderer by `library` field
- `agentService.ts` + `AgentContext.tsx` — Send `chart_library` in every chat POST, parse `library`/`spec` from SSE
- `types.ts` — Added `library` and `spec` fields to chart_spec type
- `package.json` — Added vega, vega-lite, vega-embed dependencies

**Backend (3 files modified):**
- `routes/chat.py` — Accept `chart_library` field with `Literal["plotly", "vegalite"]` validation
- `agent.py` — Dynamic system prompt: Plotly vs Vega-Lite charting instructions
- `mcp_sse.py` — `render_chart` MCP tool supports both Plotly (`data`+`layout`) and Vega-Lite (`spec`) inputs

**Code review fixes applied:**
- Proper vega-embed cleanup on unmount/re-render (memory leak fix)
- Error state reset on spec change
- Optional `data` field in chart_spec type
- Pydantic Literal validation
